    def main_control(self):
        """0000-MAIN-CONTROL."""
        self.initialization()  # PERFORM 1000-INITIALIZATION
        self.process_banking()  # PERFORM 2000-PROCESS-BANKING
        self.process_loans()  # PERFORM 3000-PROCESS-LOANS
        self.process_insurance()  # PERFORM 4000-PROCESS-INSURANCE
        self.process_investments()  # PERFORM 5000-PROCESS-INVESTMENTS
        self.generate_reports()  # PERFORM 6000-GENERATE-REPORTS
        self.termination()  # PERFORM 9000-TERMINATION
        print("STOP RUN.")

    def initialization(self):
        """1000-INITIALIZATION."""
        self.open_files()  # PERFORM 1100-OPEN-FILES
        self.initialize_counters()  # PERFORM 1200-INITIALIZE-COUNTERS
        self.get_current_date()  # PERFORM 1300-GET-CURRENT-DATE
        self.load_parameters()  # PERFORM 1400-LOAD-PARAMETERS
        self.validate_system()  # PERFORM 1500-VALIDATE-SYSTEM
        print("MEGA-ENTERPRISE SYSTEM INITIALIZED")  # DISPLAY "MEGA-ENTERPRISE SYSTEM INITIALIZED".

    def open_files(self):
        """1100-OPEN-FILES."""
        print("OPEN INPUT CUSTOMER-MASTER")  # OPEN INPUT CUSTOMER-MASTER
        print("OPEN I-O ACCOUNT-MASTER")  # OPEN I-O ACCOUNT-MASTER
        print("OPEN I-O LOAN-MASTER")  # OPEN I-O LOAN-MASTER

from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Optional

@dataclass
class CustomerMaster:
    CUST_CREDIT_SCORE: Optional[int] = None
    CUST_TOTAL_LOANS: Optional[Decimal] = None
    CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE: Optional[Decimal] = None
    CUST_RISK_RATING: Optional[str] = None

@dataclass
class AccountMaster:
    ACCT_OVERDRAFT_LIMIT: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ACCT_BALANCE: Optional[Decimal] = None

@dataclass
class LoanMaster:
    LOAN_DELINQUENT: Optional[bool] = False
    LOAN_PAYMENT_AMOUNT: Optional[Decimal] = None
    LOAN_COLLATERAL_VALUE: Optional[Decimal] = None
    LOAN_CURRENT_BALANCE: Optional[Decimal] = None
    LOAN_LTV_RATIO: Optional[Decimal] = None

class InsuranceMaster:
    INS_LIFE: Optional[bool] = False
    INS_HEALTH: Optional[bool] = False
    INS_AUTO: Optional[bool] = False
    INS_HOME: Optional[bool] = False
    INS_UMBRELLA: Optional[bool] = False
    INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT: Optional[Decimal] = None
    INS_CLAIMS_COUNT: Optional[int] = None
    INS_PREMIUM_AMOUNT: Optional[Decimal] = None

    INV_STOCKS: Optional[bool] = False

    def calculate_premiums(self):
        """4200-CALCULATE-PREMIUMS.
           DISPLAY "CALCULATING PREMIUMS..."
           SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
               READ INSURANCE-MASTER NEXT
                   AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
                   NOT AT END
                       PERFORM 4210-DETERMINE-BASE-PREMIUM
                       PERFORM 4220-APPLY-RISK-FACTOR
                       PERFORM 4230-CALCULATE-FINAL-PREMIUM
               END-READ
           END-PERFORM."""
        print("CALCULATING PREMIUMS...")
        self.ws_not_eof = True
        while not self.ws_eof:
            # Assuming a method to read INSURANCE-MASTER
            insurance_record = self.read_insurance_master_next()
            if insurance_record is None:
                self.ws_eof = True
            else:
                self.determine_base_premium()
                self.apply_risk_factor()
                self.calculate_final_premium()

    def determine_base_premium(self):
        """4210-DETERMINE-BASE-PREMIUM.
           EVALUATE TRUE
               WHEN INS-LIFE
                   COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = 
                       INS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT / 1000 * WS-LIFE-RATE-PER-1000
               WHEN INS-HEALTH
                   MOVE WS-HEALTH-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-CALC-AMOUNT
               WHEN INS-AUTO
                   MOVE WS-AUTO-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-CALC-AMOUNT
               WHEN INS-HOME
                   COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = 
                       INS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT / 1000 * WS-HOME-RATE-PER-1000
               WHEN INS-UMBRELLA
                   MOVE WS-UMBRELLA-RATE TO WS-CALC-AMOUNT
           END-EVALUATE."""
        match True:
            case self.ins_life:
                self.ws_calc_amount = (
                    self.ins_coverage_amount / Decimal("1000") * self.ws_life_rate_per_1000
                )
            case self.ins_health:
                self.ws_calc_amount = self.ws_health_base_premium
            case self.ins_auto:
                self.ws_calc_amount = self.ws_auto_base_premium
            case self.ins_home:
                self.ws_calc_amount = (
                    self.ins_coverage_amount / Decimal("1000") * self.ws_home_rate_per_1000
                )
            case self.ins_umbrella:
                self.ws_calc_amount = self.ws_umbrella_rate

    def apply_risk_factor(self):
        """4220-APPLY-RISK-FACTOR.
           IF INS-CLAIMS-COUNT > 2
               COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = WS-CALC-AMOUNT * 1.25
           END-IF."""
        if self.ins_claims_count > 2:
            self.ws_calc_amount = self.ws_calc_amount * Decimal("1.25")

    def calculate_final_premium(self):

    def calculate_position_value(self):
        """5210-CALCULATE-POSITION-VALUE.
           COMPUTE INV-MARKET-VALUE = 
               INV-QUANTITY * INV-CURRENT-PRICE."""
        self.inv_market_value = self.inv_quantity * self.inv_current_price

    def calculate_gain_loss(self):
        """5220-CALCULATE-GAIN-LOSS.
           COMPUTE INV-GAIN-LOSS = 
               INV-MARKET-VALUE - (INV-QUANTITY * INV-PURCHASE-PRICE)."""
        self.inv_gain_loss = self.inv_market_value - (self.inv_quantity * self.inv_purchase_price)

           CONTINUE."""
        pass

        """6700-MANAGEMENT-REPORTS.
           DISPLAY "GENERATING MANAGEMENT REPORTS..."
           CONTINUE."""
        print("GENERATING MANAGEMENT REPORTS...")

    def utility_procedures(self):
        """8000-UTILITY-PROCEDURES.
           CONTINUE."""
        pass

    def write_transaction(self):
        """8100-WRITE-TRANSACTION.
           MOVE WS-CURRENT-TIMESTAMP TO TRAN-TIMESTAMP
           MOVE 'DEP' TO TRAN-TYPE
           MOVE WS-CALC-AMOUNT TO TRAN-AMOUNT
           MOVE 'C' TO TRAN-STATUS
           WRITE TRANSACTION-RECORD."""
        self.tran_timestamp = self.ws_current_timestamp
        self.tran_type = 'DEP'
        self.tran_amount = self.ws_calc_amount
        self.tran_status = 'C'
        # Assuming a method to write TRANSACTION-RECORD
        self.write_transaction_record()

    def write_transaction_record(self):
        """Placeholder for writing to transaction record"""
        pass

    def write_audit(self):
        """8200-WRITE-AUDIT.
           MOVE WS-CURRENT-TIMESTAMP TO AUD-TIMESTAMP
           WRITE AUDIT-RECORD."""
        self.aud_timestamp = self.ws_current_timestamp
        # Assuming a method to write AUDIT-RECORD
        self.write_audit_record()

    def write_audit_record(self):
        """Placeholder for writing to audit record"""
        pass

    def format_date(self):
        """8300-FORMAT-DATE.
           STRING WS-TEMP-DATE(1:4) DELIMITED SIZE
                  '-' DELIMITED SIZE
                  WS-TEMP-DATE(5:2) DELIMITED SIZE
                  '-' DELIMITED SIZE
                  WS-TEMP-DATE(7:2) DELIMITED SIZE
                  INTO WS-FORMATTED-DATE."""
        self.ws_formatted_date = (
            f"{self.ws_temp_date[0:4]}-{self.ws_temp_date[4:6]}-{self.ws_temp_date[6:8]}"
        )

    def validate_account(self):
        """8400-VALIDATE-ACCOUNT.
           SET WS-VALID TO TRUE
           IF ACCT-ID = SPACES
               SET WS-INVALID TO TRUE
           END-IF."""
        self.ws_valid = True
        if self.acct_id == "":
            self.ws_invalid = True

    def calculate_tax(self):
        """8500-CALCULATE-TAX.
           EVALUATE TRUE
               WHEN WS-CALC-AMOUNT <= WS-BRACKET-1-MAX
                   COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
                       WS-CALC-AMOUNT * WS-BRACKET-1-RATE
               WHEN WS-CALC-AMOUNT <= WS-BRACKET-2-MAX
                   COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
                       (WS-BRACKET-1-MAX * WS-BRACKET-1-RATE) +
                       ((WS-CALC-AMOUNT - WS-BRACKET-1-MAX) * 
                        WS-BRACKET-2-RATE)
               WHEN WS-CALC-AMOUNT <= WS-BRACKET-3-MAX
                   COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
                       (WS-BRACKET-1-MAX * WS-BRACKET-1-RATE) +
                       ((WS-BRACKET-2-MAX - WS-BRACKET-1-MAX) * 
                        WS-BRACKET-2-RATE) +
                       ((WS-CALC-AMOUNT - WS-BRACKET-2-MAX) * 
                        WS-BRACKET-3-RATE)
               WHEN OTHER
                   COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
                       WS-CALC-AMOUNT * WS-BRACKET-5-RATE
           END-EVALUATE."""
        match True:
            case self.ws_calc_amount <= self.ws_bracket_1_max:
                self.ws_calc_tax = self.ws_calc_amount * self.ws_bracket_1_rate
            case self.ws_calc_amount <= self.ws_bracket_2_max:
                self.ws_calc_tax = (
                    (self.ws_bracket_1_max * self.ws_bracket_1_rate)
                    + ((self.ws_calc_amount - self.ws_bracket_1_max) * self.ws_bracket_2_rate)
                )
            case self.ws_calc_amount <= self.ws_bracket_3_max:
                self.ws_calc_tax = (
                    (self.ws_bracket_1_max * self.ws_bracket_1_rate)
                    + ((self.ws_bracket_2_max - self.ws_bracket_1_max) * self.ws_bracket_2_rate)
                    + ((self.ws_calc_amount - self.ws_bracket_2_max) * self.ws_bracket_3_rate)
                )
            case _:
                self.ws_calc_tax = self.ws_calc_amount * self.ws_bracket_5_rate

    def termination(self):
        """9000-TERMINATION.
           PERFORM 9100-CLOSE-FILES
           PERFORM 9200-DISPLAY-STATISTICS
           DISPLAY "MEGA-ENTERPRISE SYSTEM TERMINATED NORMALLY"."""
        self.close_files()
        self.display_statistics()
        print("MEGA-ENTERPRISE SYSTEM TERMINATED NORMALLY")

    def close_files(self):
        """9100-CLOSE-FILES.
           CLOSE CUSTOMER-MASTER
           CLOSE ACCOUNT-MASTER
           CLOSE LOAN-MASTER
           CLOSE INSURANCE-MASTER
           CLOSE INVESTMENT-MASTER
           CLOSE TRANSACTION-LOG

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Optional

@dataclass
class CustomerMaster:
    """Represents the CUSTOMER-MASTER record."""
    cust_id: str = ""
    cust_name: str = ""
    cust_last_name: str = ""
    cust_state: str = ""
    cust_credit_score: int = 0
    cust_total_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    cust_total_loans: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    cust_total_investments: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    cust_last_activity: int = 0
    loan_delinquent: bool = False

# Working storage variables
WS_ANNUAL_FEE_CARD: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_TOTAL_FEES: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_WIRE_FEE_DOMESTIC: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_WIRE_FEE_INTL: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_CALC_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_CALC_RESULT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_SAVINGS_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_PERSONAL_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_TEMP_CODE: str = ""
WS_ERROR_COUNT: int = 0
WS_PROCESS_COUNT: int = 0
WS_CURRENT_DATE: int = 0  # Assuming YYYYMMDD format
ACCT_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ACCT_MIN_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_NOT_APPROVED: bool = False
WS_NOT_EOF: bool = False
WS_EOF: bool = False
CUSTOMER_MASTER: CustomerMaster = CustomerMaster()

def customer_segmentation() -> None:
    """9310-CUSTOMER-SEGMENTATION.
    DISPLAY "SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS..."
    SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
    PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
        READ CUSTOMER-MASTER NEXT
            AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
            NOT AT END
                PERFORM 9311-CALCULATE-CLV
                PERFORM 9312-ASSIGN-SEGMENT
        END-READ
    END-PERFORM."""
    print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...")
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF, CUSTOMER_MASTER
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    WS_EOF = False
    while not WS_EOF:
        try:
            #Simulate reading CUSTOMER-MASTER
            #For testing, let's iterate 3 times before setting EOF to True
            if WS_PROCESS_COUNT > 3:
                WS_EOF = True
                raise EOFError  # Simulate end of file

            calculate_clv()
            assign_segment()
        except EOFError:
            WS_EOF = True

def calculate_clv() -> None:
    """9311-CALCULATE-CLV.
    COMPUTE WS-CALC-RESULT =
        (CUST-TOTAL-BALANCE * WS-SAVINGS-RATE) +
        (CUST-TOTAL-LOANS * WS-PERSONAL-RATE) +
        (CUST-TOTAL-INVESTMENTS * 0.01)."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, CUSTOMER_MASTER
    WS_CALC_RESULT = (
        (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_balance * WS_SAVINGS_RATE) +
        (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_loans * WS_PERSONAL_RATE) +
        (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_investments * Decimal("0.01"))
    )

def assign_segment() -> None:
    """9312-ASSIGN-SEGMENT.
    EVALUATE TRUE
        WHEN WS-CALC-RESULT > 10000
            MOVE 'PLATINUM' TO WS-TEMP-CODE
        WHEN WS-CALC-RESULT > 5000
            MOVE 'GOLD' TO WS-TEMP-CODE
        WHEN WS-CALC-RESULT > 1000
            MOVE 'SILVER' TO WS-TEMP-CODE
        WHEN OTHER
            MOVE 'BRONZE' TO WS-TEMP-CODE
    END-EVALUATE."""
    global WS_TEMP_CODE, WS_CALC_RESULT
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > 10000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'PLATINUM'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 5000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'GOLD'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 1000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'SILVER'
    else:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'BRONZE'

def disaster_recovery() -> None:
    """9450-DISASTER-RECOVERY.

def sweep_accounts() -> None:
    """9632-SWEEP-ACCOUNTS.
    IF ACCT-BALANCE > ACCT-MIN-BALANCE
        COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = ACCT-BALANCE - ACCT-MIN-BALANCE
        SUBTRACT WS-CALC-AMOUNT FROM ACCT-BALANCE
        ADD WS-CALC-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-INVESTMENTS
    END-IF."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT, ACCT_BALANCE, WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS
    if ACCT_BALANCE > ACCT_MIN_BALANCE:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = ACCT_BALANCE - ACCT_MIN_BALANCE
        ACCT_BALANCE -= WS_CALC_AMOUNT
        WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += WS_CALC_AMOUNT

    CONTINUE."""
    section_continue()

from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Optional


@dataclass
class TransactionLog:
    """Data structure for TRANSACTION-LOG"""
    tran_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

@dataclass
class Customer:
    """Data structure for Customer data."""
    cust_ssn: str = ""
    cust_credit_score: int = 0
    cust_total_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    cust_total_loans: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    cust_risk_rating: str = ""


# Initialize global variables
cust_status: str = ""
ws_temp_code: str = ""
ws_calc_result: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_calc_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_total_deposits: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_total_loans: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_total_fees: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_process_count: int = 0
ws_error_count: int = 0
ws_eof: bool = False
ws_not_eof: bool = False
ws_atm_fee_foreign: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_formatted_count: str = ""
ws_cust_count: int = 0
ws_current_timestamp: str = ""
ws_temp_string: str = ""
ws_valid: bool = False
loan_current_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
loan_paid_off: bool = False

# Initialize Customer and TransactionLog records
customer = Customer()
transaction_log = TransactionLog()

def c100_transaction_monitoring() -> None:
    """C100-TRANSACTION-MONITORING."""
    global ws_eof, ws_not_eof
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...")  # DISPLAY "MONITORING TRANSACTIONS..."
    ws_not_eof = True  # SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
    while not ws_eof: # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
        read_transaction_log()
        if not ws_eof: # NOT AT END
            c110_rule_based_detection() # PERFORM C110-RULE-BASED-DETECTION
            c120_behavior_analysis() # PERFORM C120-BEHAVIOR-ANALYSIS
            c130_network_analysis() # PERFORM C130-NETWORK-ANALYSIS


def read_transaction_log() -> None:
    """Simulates reading the next transaction from TRANSACTION-LOG"""
    global ws_eof, transaction_log
    # Dummy implementation: Sets ws_eof to True after a single "read"
    ws_eof = True


def c110_rule_based_detection() -> None:
    """C110-RULE-BASED-DETECTION."""
    if transaction_log.tran_amount >= 10000:  # IF TRAN-AMOUNT >= 10000
        c111_flag_ctr()  # PERFORM C111-FLAG-CTR
    if 5000 <= transaction_log.tran_amount < 10000:  # IF TRAN-AMOUNT >= 5000 AND TRAN-AMOUNT < 10000
        c112_check_structuring()  # PERFORM C112-CHECK-STRUCTURING


def c111_flag_ctr() -> None:
    """C111-FLAG-CTR."""
    global ws_process_count
    ws_process_count += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-PROCESS-COUNT.


def c112_check_structuring() -> None:
    """C112-CHECK-STRUCTURING."""
    global ws_error_count
    ws_error_count += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-ERROR-COUNT.

def d110_classification() -> None:
    """D110-CLASSIFICATION."""
    global customer
    if customer.cust_credit_score > 750:  # IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE > 750
        customer.cust_risk_rating = 'A'  # MOVE 'A' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    elif customer.cust_credit_score > 650:  # ELSE IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE > 650
        customer.cust_risk_rating = 'B'  # MOVE 'B' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    elif customer.cust_credit_score > 550:  # ELSE IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE > 550
        customer.cust_risk_rating = 'C'  # MOVE 'C' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    else:  # ELSE
        customer.cust_risk_rating = 'D'  # MOVE 'D' TO CUST-RISK-RATING


def d120_regression() -> None:
    """D120-REGRESSION."""
    global ws_calc_result
    ws_calc_result = (customer.cust_credit_score * 10) + \
                    (customer.cust_total_balance / 1000) - \
                    (customer.cust_total_loans / 2000)  # COMPUTE WS-CALC-RESULT = (CUST-CREDIT-SCORE * 10) + (CUST-TOTAL-BALANCE / 1000) - (CUST-TOTAL-LOANS / 2000).

    """D130-CLUSTERING."""
    pass  # CONTINUE

def main_control():
    """0000-MAIN-CONTROL."""
    initialization()  # PERFORM 1000-INITIALIZATION
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # PERFORM 2000-PROCESS-TRANSACTIONS UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
        process_transactions()
    finalization()  # PERFORM 9000-FINALIZATION
    print("STOP RUN")  # STOP RUN

def initialization():
    """1000-INITIALIZATION."""
    global WS_CURRENT_DATETIME
    global RPT_YEAR, RPT_MONTH, RPT_DAY
    
    WS_WORK_AREAS.clear() # INITIALIZE WS-WORK-AREAS
    WS_COUNTERS.clear()   # INITIALIZE WS-COUNTERS
    WS_TOTALS.clear()     # INITIALIZE WS-TOTALS
    WS_CURRENT_DATETIME = str(datetime.now()) # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-CURRENT-DATETIME
    RPT_YEAR  = str(datetime.now().year) # MOVE WS-CURR-YEAR  TO RPT-YEAR
    RPT_MONTH = str(datetime.now().month) # MOVE WS-CURR-MONTH TO RPT-MONTH
    RPT_DAY   = str(datetime.now().day)   # MOVE WS-CURR-DAY   TO RPT-DAY
    open_files()       # PERFORM 1100-OPEN-FILES
    read_parameters()  # PERFORM 1200-READ-PARAMETERS
    initialize_tables()# PERFORM 1300-INITIALIZE-TABLES
    load_reference_data()# PERFORM 1400-LOAD-REFERENCE-DATA

def open_files():
    """1100-OPEN-FILES."""
    global WS_FILE_STATUS, WS_ERROR_MSG
    
    # Mock file operations
    try:
        CUSTOMER_FILE = "customer_file"  # OPEN INPUT  CUSTOMER-FILE
        ACCOUNT_FILE = "account_file"    # OPEN INPUT  ACCOUNT-FILE
        TRANSACTION_FILE = "transaction_file" # OPEN INPUT  TRANSACTION-FILE
        REPORT_FILE = "report_file"      # OPEN OUTPUT REPORT-FILE
        ERROR_FILE = "error_file"        # OPEN OUTPUT ERROR-FILE
        MASTER_FILE = "master_file"      # OPEN I-O    MASTER-FILE
        WS_FILE_STATUS = '00'
    except Exception:
        WS_FILE_STATUS = '99'

    if WS_FILE_STATUS != '00':  # IF WS-FILE-STATUS NOT = '00'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'FILE OPEN ERROR'  # MOVE 'FILE OPEN ERROR' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
        abort_process()  # PERFORM 9500-ABORT-PROCESS

def read_parameters():
    """1200-READ-PARAMETERS."""
    global WS_PARAM_DATE, WS_PARAM_TIME, WS_JOB_ID, WS_ENV_TYPE, WS_PROCESS_DATE
    
    WS_PARAM_DATE = str(datetime.now().date().strftime("%Y%m%d")) # ACCEPT WS-PARAM-DATE FROM DATE
    WS_PARAM_TIME = str(datetime.now().time().strftime("%H%M%S")) # ACCEPT WS-PARAM-TIME FROM TIME
    WS_JOB_ID = 'BATCH-001'  # MOVE 'BATCH-001' TO WS-JOB-ID
    WS_ENV_TYPE = 'PRODUCTION'  # MOVE 'PRODUCTION' TO WS-ENV-TYPE
    WS_PROCESS_DATE = int(datetime.now().date().toordinal()) # COMPUTE WS-PROCESS-DATE = FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-PARAM-DATE).

def initialize_tables():
    """1300-INITIALIZE-TABLES."""
    global WS_TBL_IDX, RATE_TABLE_ENTRY, BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY
    
    for WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 101): # PERFORM VARYING WS-TBL-IDX FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-TBL-IDX > 100
        RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX-1] = RateTableEntry() # INITIALIZE RATE-TABLE-ENTRY(WS-TBL-IDX)
        RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX-1].RT_RATE = Decimal("0.00") # MOVE ZEROES TO RT-RATE(WS-TBL-IDX)
        RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX-1].RT_CODE = "" # MOVE SPACES TO RT-CODE(WS-TBL-IDX)
    
    for WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 51): # PERFORM VARYING WS-TBL-IDX FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-TBL-IDX > 50
        BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX-1] = BranchTableEntry() # INITIALIZE BRANCH-TABLE-ENTRY(WS-TBL-IDX)

def load_reference_data():
    """1400-LOAD-REFERENCE-DATA."""
    global WS_TBL_IDX, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_REF_RECORD, RATE_TABLE_ENTRY
    
    WS_TBL_IDX = 1  # MOVE 1 TO WS-TBL-IDX
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y' and WS_TBL_IDX <= 100:  # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y' OR WS-TBL-IDX > 100
        try:
            WS_REF_RECORD = ReferenceRecord(WS_REF_CODE = "REFCODE", WS_REF_RATE = Decimal("1.23")) #Mock
            #Simulate reading from reference file
            WS_REF_RECORD.WS_REF_CODE = "REF" + str(WS_TBL_IDX)
            WS_REF_RECORD.WS_REF_RATE = Decimal(str(WS_TBL_IDX) + ".99")
        except Exception:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            continue
        RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX-1].RT_CODE = WS_REF_RECORD.WS_REF_CODE  # MOVE WS-REF-CODE TO RT-CODE(WS-TBL-IDX)
        RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX-1].RT_RATE = WS_REF_RECORD.WS_REF_RATE  # MOVE WS-REF-RATE TO RT-RATE(WS-TBL-IDX)
        WS_TBL_IDX += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-TBL-IDX

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

def process_transactions():
    """2000-PROCESS-TRANSACTIONS."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_TRANS_COUNT, WS_TRANSACTION_REC, TRANSACTION_FILE

    try:
        WS_TRANSACTION_REC = WSTransactionRec(TXN_ACCOUNT_ID="12345", TXN_AMOUNT=Decimal("100.00"), TXN_TYPE="D") #Mock Transaction
    except Exception:
        WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
        return

    WS_TRANS_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-TRANS-COUNT
    validate_transaction() # PERFORM 2100-VALIDATE-TRANSACTION
    if WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':  # IF WS-VALID-FLAG = 'Y'
        process_by_type()  # PERFORM 2200-PROCESS-BY-TYPE
    else:
        handle_error()   # PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR

def validate_transaction():
    """2100-VALIDATE-TRANSACTION."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG, WS_ERROR_MSG, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID, TXN_AMOUNT, TXN_TYPE
    
    WS_VALID_FLAG = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
    if WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID == '' or WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID is None: # IF TXN-ACCOUNT-ID = SPACES OR LOW-VALUES
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID' # MOVE 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
        return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
    
    try:
        decimal_amount = Decimal(str(WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT))
    except:
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID AMOUNT' # MOVE 'INVALID AMOUNT' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
        return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH

    valid_txn_types = ('D', 'W', 'T', 'I')
    if WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TYPE not in valid_txn_types:  # IF TXN-TYPE NOT = 'D' AND TXN-TYPE NOT = 'W' AND TXN-TYPE NOT = 'T' AND TXN-TYPE NOT = 'I'
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE' # MOVE 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
    
    validate_account_exists() # PERFORM 2150-VALIDATE-ACCOUNT-EXISTS
    validate_business_rules() # PERFORM 2160-VALIDATE-BUSINESS-RULES

def validate_account_exists():
    """2150-VALIDATE-ACCOUNT-EXISTS."""
    global WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG, WS_ERROR_MSG, WS_VALID_FLAG

    WS_SEARCH_KEY = WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID  # MOVE TXN-ACCOUNT-ID TO WS-SEARCH-KEY
    search_account()  # PERFORM 5000-SEARCH-ACCOUNT
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'N':  # IF WS-FOUND-FLAG = 'N'
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'  # MOVE 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND' TO WS-ERROR-MSG

def validate_business_rules():
    """2160-VALIDATE-BUSINESS-RULES."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG, WS_ERROR_MSG, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, TXN_AMOUNT

    if WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TYPE == 'W':  # IF TXN-TYPE = 'W'
        if WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:  # IF TXN-AMOUNT > WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
            WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'  # MOVE 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS' TO WS-ERROR-MSG

    if WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT > Decimal('1000000'):  # IF TXN-AMOUNT > 1000000
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT'  # MOVE 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT' TO WS-ERROR-MSG

def process_by_type():
    """2200-PROCESS-BY-TYPE."""
    txn_type = WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TYPE
    match txn_type:
        case 'D':
            process_deposit()  # PERFORM 2300-PROCESS-DEPOSIT
        case 'W':
            process_withdrawal() # PERFORM 2400-PROCESS-WITHDRAWAL
        case 'T':
            process_transfer() # PERFORM 2500-PROCESS-TRANSFER
        case 'I':
            process_interest() # PERFORM 2600-PROCESS-INTEREST
        case _:
            handle_error()   # PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR

def process_deposit():
    """2300-PROCESS-DEPOSIT."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_TXN_DESC, WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS, WS_DEPOSIT_COUNT

    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
    WS_TXN_DESC = 'DEPOSIT'  # MOVE 'DEPOSIT' TO WS-TXN-DESC
    WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS += WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS
    WS_DEPOSIT_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-DEPOSIT-COUNT
    update_account()   # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
    write_audit_trail()# PERFORM 2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL

def update_account():
    """2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, ACCT_BALANCE, WS_FILE_STATUS, WS_ERROR_MSG

    ACCT_BALANCE = WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE  # MOVE WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE TO ACCT-BALANCE
    ACCT_LAST_UPDATE = str(datetime.now().date())# MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO ACCT-LAST-UPDATE

    if WS_FILE_STATUS != '00':  # IF WS-FILE-STATUS NOT = '00'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'UPDATE FAILED'  # MOVE 'UPDATE FAILED' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
        handle_error()   # PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR

def write_audit_trail():
    """2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL."""
    global WS_AUDIT_RECORD
    global TXN_ACCOUNT_ID, TXN_AMOUNT, TXN_TYPE, WS_JOB_ID
    
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD = AuditRecord() # INITIALIZE WS-AUDIT-RECORD
    AUDIT_ACCOUNT = WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID  # MOVE TXN-ACCOUNT-ID TO AUDIT-ACCOUNT
    AUDIT_AMOUNT = WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT # MOVE TXN-AMOUNT TO AUDIT-AMOUNT
    AUDIT_TYPE = WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TYPE    # MOVE TXN-TYPE TO AUDIT-TYPE
    AUDIT_TIMESTAMP = str(datetime.now())# MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO AUDIT-TIMESTAMP
    AUDIT_JOB_ID = WS_JOB_ID       # MOVE WS-JOB-ID TO AUDIT-JOB-ID
    # WRITE AUDIT-RECORD FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD

def process_withdrawal():
    """2400-PROCESS-WITHDRAWAL."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_TXN_DESC, WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS, WS_WITHDRAWAL_COUNT

    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT  # SUBTRACT TXN-AMOUNT FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
    WS_TXN_DESC = 'WITHDRAWAL'  # MOVE 'WITHDRAWAL' TO WS-TXN-DESC
    WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS += WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-WITHDRAWALS
    WS_WITHDRAWAL_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-WITHDRAWAL-COUNT
    update_account()   # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
    write_audit_trail()# PERFORM 2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL
    if WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE < WS_MIN_BALANCE_LIMIT:  # IF WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE < WS-MIN-BALANCE-LIMIT
        generate_low_balance_alert()  # PERFORM 2450-GENERATE-LOW-BALANCE-ALERT

def generate_low_balance_alert():
    """2450-GENERATE-LOW-BALANCE-ALERT."""
    global WS_ALERT_RECORD, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_ALERT_COUNT
    
    WS_ALERT_RECORD = AlertRecord() # INITIALIZE WS-ALERT-RECORD
    ALERT_TYPE = 'LOW-BAL'        # MOVE 'LOW-BAL' TO ALERT-TYPE
    ALERT_ACCOUNT = WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID  # MOVE TXN-ACCOUNT-ID TO ALERT-ACCOUNT
    ALERT_BALANCE = WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE  # MOVE WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE TO ALERT-BALANCE
    ALERT_DATE = str(datetime.now().date()) # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO ALERT-DATE
    # WRITE ALERT-RECORD FROM WS-ALERT-RECORD
    WS_ALERT_COUNT += 1           # ADD 1 TO WS-ALERT-COUNT

def process_transfer():
    """2500-PROCESS-TRANSFER."""
    validate_target_account()  # PERFORM 2510-VALIDATE-TARGET-ACCOUNT
    if WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':  # IF WS-VALID-FLAG = 'Y'
        debit_source()  # PERFORM 2520-DEBIT-SOURCE
        credit_target() # PERFORM 2530-CREDIT-TARGET
        record_transfer()# PERFORM 2540-RECORD-TRANSFER
    else:
        handle_error()   # PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR

def validate_target_account():
    """2510-VALIDATE-TARGET-ACCOUNT."""
    global WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG, WS_ERROR_MSG, WS_VALID_FLAG, TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT
    
    WS_SEARCH_KEY = WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT # MOVE TXN-TARGET-ACCOUNT TO WS-SEARCH-KEY
    search_account()  # PERFORM 5000-SEARCH-ACCOUNT
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'N':  # IF WS-FOUND-FLAG = 'N'
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'TARGET ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'  # MOVE 'TARGET ACCOUNT NOT FOUND' TO WS-ERROR-MSG

def debit_source():
    """2520-DEBIT-SOURCE."""
    global WS_SOURCE_BALANCE, ACCT_BALANCE
    
    WS_SOURCE_BALANCE -= WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT  # SUBTRACT TXN-AMOUNT FROM WS-SOURCE-BALANCE
    ACCT_BALANCE = WS_SOURCE_BALANCE  # MOVE WS-SOURCE-BALANCE TO ACCT-BALANCE
    # REWRITE ACCOUNT-RECORD

def credit_target():
    """2530-CREDIT-TARGET."""
    global WS_TARGET_BALANCE, ACCT_ID
    
    WS_TARGET_BALANCE += WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-TARGET-BALANCE
    ACCT_ID = WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT  # MOVE TXN-TARGET-ACCOUNT TO ACCT-ID
    #READ MASTER-FILE INTO WS-ACCOUNT-REC
    ACCT_BALANCE = WS_TARGET_BALANCE  # MOVE WS-TARGET-BALANCE TO ACCT-BALANCE
    #REWRITE ACCOUNT-RECORD

def record_transfer():
    """2540-RECORD-TRANSFER."""
    global WS_TOTAL_TRANSFERS, WS_TRANSFER_COUNT
    
    WS_TOTAL_TRANSFERS += WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-TRANSFERS
    WS_TRANSFER_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-TRANSFER-COUNT
    write_audit_trail()# PERFORM 2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL

def process_interest():
    """2600-PROCESS-INTEREST."""
    global WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_TXN_DESC, WS_TOTAL_INTEREST, WS_INTEREST_COUNT, WS_INTEREST_RATE
    
    WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT = WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE * WS_INTEREST_RATE / 100  # COMPUTE WS-INTEREST-AMOUNT = WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE * WS-INTEREST-RATE / 100
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT  # ADD WS-INTEREST-AMOUNT TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
    WS_TXN_DESC = 'INTEREST'  # MOVE 'INTEREST' TO WS-TXN-DESC
    WS_TOTAL_INTEREST += WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT  # ADD WS-INTEREST-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-INTEREST
    WS_INTEREST_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-INTEREST-COUNT
    update_account()   # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
    write_audit_trail()# PERFORM 2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL

def handle_error():
    """2900-HANDLE-ERROR."""
    global WS_ERROR_COUNT, WS_ERROR_RECORD, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID, WS_ERROR_MSG, WS_MAX_ERRORS, WS_ABORT_REASON

    WS_ERROR_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-ERROR-COUNT
    WS_ERROR_RECORD = ErrorRecord()  # INITIALIZE WS-ERROR-RECORD
    ERR_ACCOUNT = WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID# MOVE TXN-ACCOUNT-ID TO ERR-ACCOUNT
    ERR_MESSAGE = WS_ERROR_MSG    # MOVE WS-ERROR-MSG TO ERR-MESSAGE
    ERR_TIMESTAMP = str(datetime.now().date())# MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO ERR-TIMESTAMP
    #WRITE ERROR-RECORD FROM WS-ERROR-RECORD
    if WS_ERROR_COUNT > WS_MAX_ERRORS:  # IF WS-ERROR-COUNT > WS-MAX-ERRORS
        WS_ABORT_REASON = 'MAX ERRORS EXCEEDED'  # MOVE 'MAX ERRORS EXCEEDED' TO WS-ABORT-REASON
        abort_process()  # PERFORM 9500-ABORT-PROCESS

def batch_processing():
    """3000-BATCH-PROCESSING."""
    load_batch_header()# PERFORM 3100-LOAD-BATCH-HEADER
    while WS_BATCH_EOF != 'Y': # PERFORM 3200-PROCESS-BATCH-ITEMS UNTIL WS-BATCH-EOF = 'Y'
        process_batch_items()
    validate_batch_totals()# PERFORM 3300-VALIDATE-BATCH-TOTALS
    commit_batch()     # PERFORM 3400-COMMIT-BATCH

def load_batch_header():
    """3100-LOAD-BATCH-HEADER."""
    global WS_BATCH_EOF, WS_CURRENT_BATCH, WS_EXPECTED_COUNT, WS_EXPECTED_TOTAL, BATCH_ID, BATCH_COUNT, BATCH_TOTAL
    
    try:
        # READ BATCH-FILE INTO WS-BATCH-HEADER
        # MOCK data reading
        BATCH_ID = "BH123"
        BATCH_COUNT = 10
        BATCH_TOTAL = Decimal("1000.00")
    except Exception:
        WS_BATCH_EOF = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-BATCH-EOF
        return

    WS_CURRENT_BATCH = BATCH_ID    # MOVE BATCH-ID TO WS-CURRENT-BATCH
    WS_EXPECTED_COUNT = BATCH_COUNT  # MOVE BATCH-COUNT TO WS-EXPECTED-COUNT
    WS_EXPECTED_TOTAL = BATCH_TOTAL  # MOVE BATCH-TOTAL TO WS-EXPECTED-TOTAL

def process_batch_items():
    """3200-PROCESS-BATCH-ITEMS."""
    global WS_BATCH_EOF, WS_ACTUAL_COUNT, WS_ACTUAL_TOTAL, ITEM_AMOUNT

    try:
        # READ BATCH-FILE INTO WS-BATCH-ITEM
        # MOCK data reading
        ITEM_AMOUNT = Decimal("50.00")
        ITEM_TYPE = "PAY"
    except Exception:
        WS_BATCH_EOF = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-BATCH-EOF
        return
    
    WS_ACTUAL_COUNT += 1   # ADD 1 TO WS-ACTUAL-COUNT
    WS_ACTUAL_TOTAL += ITEM_AMOUNT  # ADD ITEM-AMOUNT TO WS-ACTUAL-TOTAL
    process_single_item()# PERFORM 3250-PROCESS-SINGLE-ITEM

def process_single_item():
    """3250-PROCESS-SINGLE-ITEM."""
    global ITEM_TYPE
    
    match ITEM_TYPE:  # EVALUATE ITEM-TYPE
        case 'PAY':
            process_payment()    # PERFORM 3260-PROCESS-PAYMENT
        case 'REF':
            process_refund()     # PERFORM

# === CHUNK 7 (lines 4009-4676) ===
```python
from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal
from datetime import date

@dataclass

    trade_rec_shares: int = 0
    trade_rec_price: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

class LoanProcessor:
    def __init__(self):
        self.ws_ltv_ratio: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_pmi_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_loan_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_late_90_days: int = 0
        self.ws_late_60_days: int = 0
        self.ws_late_30_days: int = 0
        self.ws_risk_score: int = 0
        self.ws_factor_1: str = ""
        self.ws_factor_2: str = ""
        self.ws_factor_3: str = ""
        self.ws_risk_category: str = ""
        self.ws_credit_tier: str = ""
        self.ws_approval_status: str = ""
        self.ws_conditions: str = ""
        self.ws_approved_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_base_rate: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_approved_rate: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_loan_interest_rate: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_monthly_rate: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_compound_factor: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_loan_principal_bal: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_running_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_payment_date: date = date(1900, 1, 1)
        self.ws_amort_idx: int = 0
        self.ws_payment_month: int = 0
        self.ws_payment_year: int = 0
        self.loan_mortgage: bool = False
        self.ws_property_tax: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_insurance_premium: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_loan_start_date: date = date(1900, 1, 1)
        self.ws_loan_end_date: date = date(1900, 1, 1)
        self.ws_loan_status: str = ""
        self.ws_loan_id: str = ""
        self.ws_loan_type: str = ""
        self.ws_disbursement_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_notif_type: str = ""
        self.ws_notif_channel: str = ""
        self.ws_notif_subject: str = ""
        self.ws_decline_record: WSDeclineRecord = WSDeclineRecord()
        self.ws_loan_record: WSLoanRecord = WSLoanRecord()
        self.amort_interest: list[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * 1000
        self.amort_principal: list[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * 1000
        self.amort_balance: list[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * 1000
        self.amort_payment_num: list[int] = [0] * 1000
        self.amort_payment_amt: list[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * 1000
        self.amort_escrow: list[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * 1000
        self.amort_total_pmt: list[Decimal] = [Decimal("0.00")] * 1000
        self.amort_payment_date: list[date] = [date(1900, 1, 1)] * 1000
        self.ws_loan_term_months: int = 360

    def calculate_pmi(self) -> None:
        """
        10335-CALCULATE-PMI.
        """
        match True:
            case self.ws_ltv_ratio > 95:
                self.ws_pmi_amount = self.ws_loan_amount * Decimal("0.0125") / 12
            case self.ws_ltv_ratio > 90:
                self.ws_pmi_amount = self.ws_loan_amount * Decimal("0.0100") / 12
            case self.ws_ltv_ratio > 85:
                self.ws_pmi_amount = self.ws_loan_amount * Decimal("0.0075") / 12
            case _:
                self.ws_pmi_amount = self.ws_loan_amount * Decimal("0.0050") / 12

    def evaluate_history(self) -> None:
        """
        10340-EVALUATE-HISTORY.
        """
        if self.ws_late_90_days > 0:
            self.ws_risk_score -= 50
            self.ws_factor_1 = 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY'
        if self.ws_late_60_days > 2:
            self.ws_risk_score -= 30
            self.ws_factor_2 = '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES'
        if self.ws_late_30_days > 5:
            self.ws_risk_score -= 20
            self.ws_factor_3 = 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES'

    def calculate_final_risk(self) -> None:
        """
        10350-CALCULATE-FINAL-RISK.
        """
        self.ws_risk_score = self.ws_risk_score / 4
        match True:
            case self.ws_risk_score >= 80:
                self.ws_risk_category = 'LOW RISK'
            case self.ws_risk_score >= 60:
                self.ws_risk_category = 'MODERATE'
            case self.ws_risk_score >= 40:
                self.ws_risk_category = 'ELEVATED'
            case _:
                self.ws_risk_category = 'HIGH RISK'

    def determine_approval(self) -> None:
        """
        10400-DETERMINE-APPROVAL.
        """
        if self.ws_credit_tier == 'F':
            self.ws_approval_status = 'D'
            self.ws_conditions = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'
            return

        if self.ws_risk_category == 'HIGH RISK':
            self.ws_approval_status = 'D'
            self.ws_conditions = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'
            return

        if self.ws_dti_ratio > 50:
            self.ws_approval_status = 'D'
            self.ws_conditions = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'
            return

        self.ws_approval_status = 'A'
        self.calculate_approved_terms()

    def calculate_approved_terms(self) -> None:
        """
        10450-CALCULATE-APPROVED-TERMS.
        """
        self.ws_approved_amount = self.ws_loan_amount
        match self.ws_credit_tier:
            case 'A':
                self.ws_approved_rate = self.ws_base_rate + Decimal("0.00")
            case 'B':
                self.ws_approved_rate = self.ws_base_rate + Decimal("0.50")
            case 'C':
                self.ws_approved_rate = self.ws_base_rate + Decimal("1.50")
            case 'D':
                self.ws_approved_rate = self.ws_base_rate + Decimal("3.00")

        if self.ws_risk_category == 'ELEVATED':
            self.ws_approved_rate += Decimal("0.50")

    def generate_loan_terms(self) -> None:
        """
        10500-GENERATE-LOAN-TERMS.
        """
        self.ws_loan_interest_rate = self.ws_approved_rate
        self.ws_monthly_rate = self.ws_loan_interest_rate / 1200
        self.ws_compound_factor = (1 + self.ws_monthly_rate) ** self.ws_loan_term_months
        self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt = (
            self.ws_loan_amount
            * self.ws_monthly_rate
            * self.ws_compound_factor
            / (self.ws_compound_factor - 1)
        )
        self.ws_loan_principal_bal = self.ws_loan_amount

    def create_amortization(self) -> None:
        """
        10600-CREATE-AMORTIZATION.
        """
        self.ws_running_balance = self.ws_loan_amount
        self.ws_payment_date = date.today()  # FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE
        self.ws_amort_idx = 1
        while self.ws_amort_idx <= self.ws_loan_term_months:
            self.calculate_payment_split()
            self.ws_amort_idx += 1

    def calculate_payment_split(self) -> None:
        """
        10650-CALCULATE-PAYMENT-SPLIT.
        """
        self.amort_interest[self.ws_amort_idx - 1] = (
            self.ws_running_balance * self.ws_monthly_rate
        )
        self.amort_principal[self.ws_amort_idx - 1] = (
            self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt - self.amort_interest[self.ws_amort_idx - 1]
        )
        self.ws_running_balance -= self.amort_principal[self.ws_amort_idx - 1]
        self.amort_balance[self.ws_amort_idx - 1] = self.ws_running_balance
        self.amort_payment_num[self.ws_amort_idx - 1] = self.ws_amort_idx
        self.amort_payment_amt[self.ws_amort_idx - 1] = self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt

        if self.loan_mortgage:
            self.amort_escrow[self.ws_amort_idx - 1] = (
                self.ws_property_tax + self.ws_insurance_premium
            ) / 12
            self.amort_total_pmt[self.ws_amort_idx - 1] = (
                self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt
                + self.amort_escrow[self.ws_amort_idx - 1]
                + self.ws_pmi_amount
            )
        else:
            self.amort_total_pmt[self.ws_amort_idx - 1] = self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt

        self.advance_payment_date()

    def advance_payment_date(self) -> None:
        """
        10660-ADVANCE-PAYMENT-DATE.
        """
        self.ws_payment_month += 1
        if self.ws_payment_month > 12:
            self.ws_payment_month = 1
            self.ws_payment_year += 1

        self.amort_payment_date[self.ws_amort_idx - 1] = date(
            self.ws_payment_year, self.ws_payment_month, 1
        )

    def finalize_loan(self) -> None:
        """
        10700-FINALIZE-LOAN.
        """
        self.ws_loan_start_date = date.today()  # FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE
        # Approximation, COBOL code calculates date arithmetic differently
        days = self.ws_loan_term_months * 30
        self.ws_loan_end_date = date(self.ws_loan_start_date.year, self.ws_loan_start_date.month, self.ws_loan_start_date.day)
        #TODO: Use datetime.timedelta to add days instead of date replacement above
        self.ws_loan_status = 'A'
        self.create_loan_record()
        self.disburse_funds()
        self.send_confirmation()

    def create_loan_record(self) -> None:
        """
        10750-CREATE-LOAN-RECORD.
        """
        self.ws_loan_record = WSLoanRecord()
        self.ws_loan_record.loan_rec_id = self.ws_loan_id
        self.ws_loan_record.loan_rec_type = self.ws_loan_type
        self.ws_loan_record.loan_rec_amount = self.ws_loan_amount
        self.ws_loan_record.loan_rec_rate = self.ws_loan_interest_rate
        self.ws_loan_record.loan_rec_payment = self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt
        self.ws_loan_record.loan_rec_start = self.ws_loan_start_date
        self.ws_loan_record.loan_rec_status = self.ws_loan_status
        # WRITE LOAN-RECORD FROM WS-LOAN-RECORD (Simulated write)
        print(f"Loan record created: {self.ws_loan_record}")

    def disburse_funds(self) -> None:
        """
        10760-DISBURSE-FUNDS.
        """
        self.ws_disbursement_amount = self.ws_loan_amount
        self.process_deposit()
        self.write_audit_trail()

    def send_confirmation(self) -> None:
        """
        10770-SEND-CONFIRMATION.
        """
        self.ws_notif_type = 'LOAN-CONFIRM'
        self.ws_notif_channel = 'EMAIL'
        self.ws_notif_subject = 'Your loan has been approved'
        self.send_notification()

    def process_decline(self) -> None:
        """
        10800-PROCESS-DECLINE.
        """
        self.ws_loan_status = 'DECLINED'
        self.record_decline()
        self.send_decline_notice()

    def record_decline(self) -> None:
        """
        10810-RECORD-DECLINE.
        """
        self.ws_decline_record = WSDeclineRecord()
        self.ws_decline_record.decline_loan_id = self.ws_loan_id
        self.ws_decline_record.decline_status = self.ws_approval_status
        self.ws_decline_record.decline_reason = self.ws_conditions
        self.ws_decline_record.decline_date = date.today()  # FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE
        # WRITE DECLINE-RECORD FROM WS-DECLINE-RECORD (Simulated write)
        print(f"Decline record created: {self.ws_decline_record}")

    def send_decline_notice(self) -> None:
        """
        10820-SEND-DECLINE-NOTICE.
        """
        self.ws_notif_type = 'LOAN-DECLINE'
        self.ws_notif_channel = 'LETTER'
        self.ws_notif_subject = 'Regarding your loan application'
        self.send_notification()

    def process_deposit(self) -> None:
        """
        2300-PROCESS-DEPOSIT (Dummy).
        """
        print("Processing deposit (dummy)")

    def write_audit_trail(self) -> None:
        """
        2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL (Dummy).
        """
        print("Writing audit trail (dummy)")

    def send_notification(self) -> None:
        """
        15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION (Dummy).
        """
        print(
            f"Sending notification: Type={self.ws_notif_type}, "
            f"Channel={self.ws_notif_channel}, Subject='{self.ws_notif_subject}'"
        )

class PortfolioManager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.ws_hold_idx: int = 0
        self.ws_eof_flag: str = ""
        self.ws_holding_rec: WSHoldingRec = WSHoldingRec()
        self.ws_holding: list[WSHoldingRec] = [WSHoldingRec()] * 100
        self.ws_holdings_count: int = 0
        self.ws_quote_symbol: str = ""
        self.ws_quote_price: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_total_value: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_cost_basis: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_unrealized_gain: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_hold_cost: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_rebalance_needed: str = ""
        self.ws_stocks_value: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_bonds_value: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_cash_value: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_stocks_pct: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_bonds_pct: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_cash_pct: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_target_stocks_pct: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_target_bonds_pct: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_stocks_diff: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_bonds_diff: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_sell_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_buy_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_trade_type: str = ""
        self.ws_order_type: str = ""
        self.ws_trade_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_end_of_quarter: str = ""
        self.ws_end_of_year: str = ""
        self.ws_quarter_start_value: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_dividend_income: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_realized_gain_ytd: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.rpt_title: str = ""
        self.rpt_quarter_return: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.rpt_dividends: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.rpt_cap_gains: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.report_record: str = "" #String placeholder
        self.ws_holdings_line: str = "" #String placeholder
        self.ws_performance_line: str = "" #String placeholder
        self.ws_tax_line: str = "" #String placeholder

        #Placeholders for file and record variables
        self.holdings_file: list[WSHoldingRec] = []
        self.quote_request: QuoteRequest = QuoteRequest()
        self.quote_response: QuoteResponse = QuoteResponse()

    def portfolio_management(self) -> None:
        """
        11000-PORTFOLIO-MANAGEMENT.
        """
        self.load_portfolio()
        self.update_market_prices()
        self.calculate_values()
        self.rebalance_check()
        self.generate_statements()

    def load_portfolio(self) -> None:
        """
        11100-LOAD-PORTFOLIO.
        """
        self.ws_hold_idx = 1
        self.ws_eof_flag = ''
        while self.ws_hold_idx <= 100 and self.ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
            try:
                self.ws_holding_rec = self.holdings_file[self.ws_hold_idx-1]
                self.ws_holding[self.ws_hold_idx - 1] = self.ws_holding_rec
                self.ws_hold_idx += 1
            except IndexError:
                self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        self.ws_holdings_count = self.ws_hold_idx - 1

    def update_market_prices(self) -> None:
        """
        11200-UPDATE-MARKET-PRICES.
        """
        self.ws_hold_idx = 1
        while self.ws_hold_idx <= self.ws_holdings_count:
            self.ws_quote_symbol = self.ws_holding[self.ws_hold_idx - 1].hold_symbol
            self.get_quote()
            self.ws_holding[self.ws_hold_idx - 1].hold_current_price = self.ws_quote_price
            self.ws_hold_idx += 1

    def get_quote(self) -> None:
        """
        11250-GET-QUOTE.
        """
        self.quote_request.quote_request_symbol = self.ws_quote_symbol
        #CALL 'GETQUOTE' USING QUOTE-REQUEST QUOTE-RESPONSE (Simulated call)
        self.quote_response = self.simulate_get_quote(self.quote_request)
        if self.quote_response.quote_response_status == 'OK':
            self.ws_quote_price = self.quote_response.quote_last_price
        else:
            self.ws_quote_price = Decimal("0.00")

    def simulate_get_quote(self, quote_request: QuoteRequest) -> QuoteResponse:
        """
        Simulates external 'GETQUOTE' call.  Returns a dummy quote.
        """
        if quote_request.quote_request_symbol == 'XYZ':
            return QuoteResponse(Decimal("123.45"), "OK")
        else:
            return QuoteResponse(Decimal("0.00"), "ERROR")

    def calculate_values(self) -> None:
        """
        11300-CALCULATE-VALUES.
        """
        self.ws_total_value = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_cost_basis = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_unrealized_gain = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_hold_idx = 1
        while self.ws_hold_idx <= self.ws_holdings_count:
            self.calculate_holding_value()
            self.ws_hold_idx += 1

    def calculate_holding_value(self) -> None:
        """
        11350-CALCULATE-HOLDING-VALUE.
        """
        holding = self.ws_holding[self.ws_hold_idx - 1]
        holding.hold_market_value = (
            Decimal(holding.hold_shares) * holding.hold_current_price
        )
        self.ws_hold_cost = (
            Decimal(holding.hold_shares) * holding.hold_cost_per_share
        )
        holding.hold_gain_loss = holding.hold_market_value - self.ws_hold_cost
        if self.ws_hold_cost > 0:
            holding.hold_pct_change = (holding.hold_gain_loss / self.ws_hold_cost) * 100
        else:
            holding.hold_pct_change = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_total_value += holding.hold_market_value
        self.ws_cost_basis += self.ws_hold_cost
        self.ws_unrealized_gain += holding.hold_gain_loss

    def rebalance_check(self) -> None:
        """
        11400-REBALANCE-CHECK.
        """
        self.calculate_current_allocation()
        self.compare_to_target()
        if self.ws_rebalance_needed == 'Y':
            self.generate_rebalance_trades()

    def calculate_current_allocation(self) -> None:
        """
        11410-CALCULATE-CURRENT-ALLOCATION.
        """
        self.ws_stocks_value = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_bonds_value = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_cash_value = Decimal("0.00")
        self.ws_hold_idx = 1
        while self.ws_hold_idx <= self.ws_holdings_count:
            match self.ws_holding[self.ws_hold_idx-1].hold_type:
                case 'STK':
                    self.ws_stocks_value += self.ws_holding[self.ws_hold_idx-1].hold_market_value
                case 'BND':
                    self.ws_bonds_value += self.ws_holding[self.ws_hold_idx-1].hold_market_value
                case 'CSH':
                    self.ws_cash_value += self.ws_holding[self.ws_hold_idx-1].hold_market_value
            self.ws_hold_idx += 1
        self.ws_stocks_pct = (self.ws_stocks_value / self.ws_total_value) * 100
        self.ws_bonds_pct = (self.ws_bonds_value / self.ws_total_value) * 100
        self.ws_cash_pct = (self.ws_cash_value / self.ws_total_value) * 100

    def compare_to_target(self) -> None:
        """
        11420-COMPARE-TO-TARGET.
        """
        self.ws_rebalance_needed = 'N'
        self.ws_stocks_diff = self.ws_stocks_pct - self.ws_target_stocks_pct
        self.ws_bonds_diff = self.ws_bonds_pct - self.ws_target_bonds_pct
        if abs(self.ws_stocks_diff) > 5:
            self.ws_rebalance_needed = 'Y'
        if abs(self.ws_bonds_diff) > 5:
            self.ws_rebalance_needed = 'Y'

    def generate_rebalance_trades(self) -> None:
        """
        11430-GENERATE-REBALANCE-TRADES.
        """
        if self.ws_stocks_diff > 0:
            self.ws_sell_amount = self.ws_total_value * self.ws_stocks_diff / 100
            self.create_sell_order()
        else:
            self.ws_buy_amount = self.ws_total_value * (0 - self.ws_stocks_diff) / 100
            self.create_buy_order()

    def create_sell_order(self) -> None:
        """
        11440-CREATE-SELL-ORDER.
        """
        self.ws_trade_type = 'SELL'
        self.ws_order_type = 'MARKET'
        self.ws_trade_amount = self.ws_sell_amount
        self.trade_execution()

    def create_buy_order(self) -> None:
        """
        11450-CREATE-BUY-ORDER.
        """
        self.ws_trade_type = 'BUY '
        self.ws_order_type = 'MARKET'
        self.ws_trade_amount = self.ws_buy_amount
        self.trade_execution()

    def generate_statements(self) -> None:
        """
        11500-GENERATE-STATEMENTS.
        """
        self.monthly_statement()
        if self.ws_end_of_quarter == 'Y':
            self.quarterly_report()
        if self.ws_end_of_year == 'Y':
            self.annual_tax_report()

    def monthly_statement(self) -> None:
        """
        11510-MONTHLY-STATEMENT.
        """
        self.rpt_title = 'MONTHLY INVESTMENT STATEMENT'
        self.write_holdings_detail()

    def write_holdings_detail(self) -> None:
        """
        11515-WRITE-HOLDINGS-DETAIL.
        """
        self.ws_hold_idx = 1
        while self.ws_hold_idx <= self.ws_holdings_count:
            holding = self.ws_holding[self.ws_hold_idx - 1]
            rpt_symbol = holding.hold_symbol
            rpt_shares = holding.hold_shares
            rpt_price = holding.hold_current_price
            rpt_value = holding.hold_market_value
            rpt_gain = holding.hold_gain_loss
            self.ws_holdings_line = f"{rpt_symbol} {rpt_shares} {rpt_price} {rpt_value} {rpt_gain}"
            self.report_record = self.ws_holdings_line
            print(f"Writing monthly statement line: {self.report_record}") #Simulating write
            self.ws_hold_idx += 1

    def quarterly_report(self) -> None:
        """
        11520-QUARTERLY-REPORT.
        """
        self.rpt_title = 'QUARTERLY PERFORMANCE REPORT'
        self.rpt_quarter_return = (
            (self.ws_total_value - self.ws_quarter_start_value) / self.ws_quarter_start_value
        ) * 100
        self.ws_performance_line = f"{self.rpt_quarter_return}"
        self.report_record = self.ws_performance_line
        print(f"Writing quarterly report line: {self.report_record}") #Simulating write

    def annual_tax_report(self) -> None:
        """
        11530-ANNUAL-TAX-REPORT.
        """
        self.rpt_title = 'ANNUAL TAX REPORT - 1099'
        self.rpt_dividends = self.ws_dividend_income
        self.rpt_cap_gains = self.ws_realized_gain_ytd
        self.ws_tax_line = f"{self.rpt_dividends} {self.rpt_cap_gains}"
        self.report_record = self.ws_tax_line
        print(f"Writing tax report line: {self.report_record}") #Simulating write

    def trade_execution(self) -> None:
        """
        12000-TRADE-EXECUTION.
        """
        self.validate_order()
        if self.ws_order_valid == 'Y':
            self.check_funds_

class CobolProgram:  # Changed to a class

    def __init__(self, storage: DataStorage, ws: WorkingStorage):
        self.storage = storage
        self.ws = ws

    def calculate_driver_premium(self):
        """
        COBOL equivalent: (No COBOL line number since it's part of a larger context)
        Calculates driver premium based on age, accidents, and violations.
        """
        match self.ws.WS_DRIVER-AGE:
            case age if 6 <= age <= 10:  # WHEN 6 THRU 10
                self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM += Decimal("100") # ADD 100 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            case _: # WHEN OTHER
                self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM += Decimal("50") # ADD 50 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM

        if self.ws.WS_DRIVER-AGE < 25: # IF WS-DRIVER-AGE < 25
            self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5") # MULTIPLY 1.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM

        if self.ws.WS_ACCIDENTS-3YR > 0: # IF WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR > 0
            self.ws.WS_ACCIDENT-SURCHARGE = self.ws.WS_ACCIDENTS-3YR * Decimal("200") # COMPUTE WS-ACCIDENT-SURCHARGE = WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR * 200
            self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM += self.ws.WS_ACCIDENT-SURCHARGE # ADD WS-ACCIDENT-SURCHARGE TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM

        if self.ws.WS_VIOLATIONS-3YR > 0: # IF WS-VIOLATIONS-3YR > 0
            self.ws.WS_VIOLATION-SURCHARGE = self.ws.WS_VIOLATIONS-3YR * Decimal("100") # COMPUTE WS-VIOLATION-SURCHARGE = WS-VIOLATIONS-3YR * 100
            self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM += self.ws.WS_VIOLATION-SURCHARGE # ADD WS-VIOLATION-SURCHARGE TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM

        self.ws.WS_ANNUAL-PREMIUM = self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM # MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM
        self.ws.WS_MONTHLY-PREMIUM = self.ws.WS_ANNUAL-PREMIUM / Decimal("12") # COMPUTE WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM = WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM / 12.

    def calc_home_premium(self):
        """13230-CALC-HOME-PREMIUM.
        Calculates home premium based on coverage amount, home age, flood zone, security system, and deductible.
        """
        self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM = self.ws.WS_COVERAGE-AMOUNT * Decimal("0.003") # COMPUTE WS-BASE-PREMIUM = WS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT * 0.003

        match self.ws.WS_HOME-AGE: # EVALUATE WS-HOME-AGE
            case age if 0 <= age <= 10: # WHEN 0 THRU 10
                self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9") # MULTIPLY 0.9 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            case age if 11 <= age <= 25: # WHEN 11 THRU 25
                self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0") # MULTIPLY 1.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            case age if 26 <= age <= 50: # WHEN 26 THRU 50
                self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.2") # MULTIPLY 1.2 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            case _: # WHEN OTHER
                self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5") # MULTIPLY 1.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM

        if self.ws.WS_FLOOD-ZONE == 'Y': # IF WS-FLOOD-ZONE = 'Y'
            self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5") # MULTIPLY 1.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM

        if self.ws.WS_SECURITY-SYSTEM == 'Y': # IF WS-SECURITY-SYSTEM = 'Y'
            self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9") # MULTIPLY 0.9 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM

        self.ws.WS_DEDUCTIBLE-CREDIT = self.ws.WS_DEDUCTIBLE / Decimal("1000") * Decimal("50") # COMPUTE WS-DEDUCTIBLE-CREDIT = WS-DEDUCTIBLE / 1000 * 50
        self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM -= self.ws.WS_DEDUCTIBLE-CREDIT # SUBTRACT WS-DEDUCTIBLE-CREDIT FROM WS-BASE-PREMIUM

        if self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM < 200: # IF WS-BASE-PREMIUM < 200
            self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM = Decimal("200") # MOVE 200 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM

        self.ws.WS_ANNUAL-PREMIUM = self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM # MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM
        self.ws.WS_MONTHLY-PREMIUM = self.ws.WS_ANNUAL-PREMIUM / Decimal("12") # COMPUTE WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM = WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM / 12.

    def calc_health_premium(self):
        """13240-CALC-HEALTH-PREMIUM.
        Calculates health premium based on insured age and plan type.
        """
        self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM = Decimal("300") # MOVE 300 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM

        match self.ws.WS_INSURED-AGE: # EVALUATE WS-INSURED-AGE
            case age if 0 <= age <= 18: # WHEN 0 THRU 18
                self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.5") # MULTIPLY 0.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            case age if 19 <= age <= 30: # WHEN 19 THRU 30
                self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0") # MULTIPLY 1.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            case age if 31 <= age <= 40: # WHEN 31 THRU 40
                self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.3") # MULTIPLY 1.3 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            case age if 41 <= age <= 50: # WHEN 41 THRU 50
                self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.6") # MULTIPLY 1.6 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            case age if 51 <= age <= 60: # WHEN 51 THRU 60
                self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.0") # MULTIPLY 2.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            case _: # WHEN OTHER
                self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.8") # MULTIPLY 2.8 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM

        match self.ws.WS_PLAN-TYPE: # EVALUATE WS-PLAN-TYPE
            case 'BRONZE': # WHEN 'BRONZE'
                self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.8") # MULTIPLY 0.8 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            case 'SILVER': # WHEN 'SILVER'
                self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0") # MULTIPLY 1.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            case 'GOLD': # WHEN 'GOLD'
                self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.3") # MULTIPLY 1.3 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
            case 'PLATINUM': # WHEN 'PLATINUM'
                self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.6") # MULTIPLY 1.6 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM

        if self.ws.WS_FAMILY-PLAN == 'Y': # IF WS-FAMILY-PLAN = 'Y'
            self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.5") # MULTIPLY 2.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM

        self.ws.WS_MONTHLY-PREMIUM = self.ws.WS_BASE-PREMIUM # MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM
        self.ws.WS_ANNUAL-PREMIUM = self.ws.WS_MONTHLY-PREMIUM * Decimal("12") # COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM = WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM * 12.

    def underwriting(self):
        """13300-UNDERWRITING."""
        self.evaluate_risk_factors() # PERFORM 13310-EVALUATE-RISK-FACTORS
        self.check_medical_history() # PERFORM 13320-CHECK-MEDICAL-HISTORY
        self.verify_information() # PERFORM 13330-VERIFY-INFORMATION
        self.determine_decision() # PERFORM 13340-DETERMINE-DECISION

    def evaluate_risk_factors(self):
        """13310-EVALUATE-RISK-FACTORS."""
        self.ws.WS_RISK-POINTS = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        if self.ws.POLICY-LIFE: # IF POLICY-LIFE
            if self.ws.WS_BMI > 30: # IF WS-BMI > 30
                self.ws.WS_RISK-POINTS += 10 # ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
            if self.ws.WS_SMOKER-FLAG == 'Y': # IF WS-SMOKER-FLAG = 'Y'
                self.ws.WS_RISK-POINTS += 25 # ADD 25 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
            if self.ws.WS_HAZARDOUS-OCCUPATION == 'Y': # IF WS-HAZARDOUS-OCCUPATION = 'Y'
                self.ws.WS_RISK-POINTS += 15 # ADD 15 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        if self.ws.POLICY-AUTO: # IF POLICY-AUTO
            if self.ws.WS_DRIVER-AGE < 21: # IF WS-DRIVER-AGE < 21
                self.ws.WS_RISK-POINTS += 20 # ADD 20 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
            if self.ws.WS_ACCIDENTS-3YR > 1: # IF WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR > 1
                self.ws.WS_RISK-POINTS += 15 # ADD 15 TO WS-RISK-POINTS

    def check_medical_history(self):
        """13320-CHECK-MEDICAL-HISTORY."""
        if self.ws.WS_CHRONIC-CONDITIONS > 0: # IF WS-CHRONIC-CONDITIONS > 0
            self.ws.WS_CONDITION-POINTS = self.ws.WS_CHRONIC-CONDITIONS * 5 # COMPUTE WS-CONDITION-POINTS = WS-CHRONIC-CONDITIONS * 5
            self.ws.WS_RISK-POINTS += self.ws.WS_CONDITION-POINTS # ADD WS-CONDITION-POINTS TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        if self.ws.WS_RECENT-HOSPITALIZATION == 'Y': # IF WS-RECENT-HOSPITALIZATION = 'Y'
            self.ws.WS_RISK-POINTS += 10 # ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        if self.ws.WS_PRESCRIPTION-COUNT > 5: # IF WS-PRESCRIPTION-COUNT > 5
            self.ws.WS_RISK-POINTS += 5 # ADD 5 TO WS-RISK-POINTS

    def verify_information(self):
        """13330-VERIFY-INFORMATION."""
        self.check_fraud_indicators() # PERFORM 13335-CHECK-FRAUD-INDICATORS
        self.validate_documents() # PERFORM 13336-VALIDATE-DOCUMENTS

    def check_fraud_indicators(self):
        """13335-CHECK-FRAUD-INDICATORS."""
        if self.ws.WS_RECENT-CLAIMS > 3: # IF WS-RECENT-CLAIMS > 3
            self.ws.WS_RISK-POINTS += 20 # ADD 20 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
            self.ws.WS_FRAUD-FLAG = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-FRAUD-FLAG
        if self.ws.WS_ADDRESS-MISMATCH == 'Y': # IF WS-ADDRESS-MISMATCH = 'Y'
            self.ws.WS_RISK-POINTS += 10 # ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS

    def validate_documents(self):
        """13336-VALIDATE-DOCUMENTS."""
        if self.ws.WS_DOC-MISSING == 'Y': # IF WS-DOC-MISSING = 'Y'
            self.ws.WS_UW-STATUS = 'PENDING' # MOVE 'PENDING' TO WS-UW-STATUS
        else: # ELSE
            self.ws.WS_UW-STATUS = 'COMPLETE' # MOVE 'COMPLETE' TO WS-UW-STATUS

    def determine_decision(self):
        """13340-DETERMINE-DECISION."""
        match True: # EVALUATE TRUE
            case bool(self.ws.WS_RISK-POINTS > 50): # WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 50
                self.ws.WS_UW-DECISION = 'DECLINE' # MOVE 'DECLINE' TO WS-UW-DECISION
            case bool(self.ws.WS_RISK-POINTS > 30): # WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 30
                self.ws.WS_UW-DECISION = 'SUBSTANDARD' # MOVE 'SUBSTANDARD' TO WS-UW-DECISION
                self.ws.WS_ANNUAL-PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5") # COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM = WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM * 1.5
            case bool(self.ws.WS_RISK-POINTS > 15): # WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 15
                self.ws.WS_UW-DECISION = 'STANDARD' # MOVE 'STANDARD' TO WS-UW-DECISION
            case _: # WHEN OTHER
                self.ws.WS_UW-DECISION = 'PREFERRED' # MOVE 'PREFERRED' TO WS-UW-DECISION
                self.ws.WS_ANNUAL-PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9") # COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM = WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM * 0.9

    def issue_policy(self):
        """13400-ISSUE-POLICY."""
        if self.ws.WS_UW-DECISION != 'DECLINE': # IF WS-UW-DECISION NOT = 'DECLINE'
            self.generate_policy_number() # PERFORM 13410-GENERATE-POLICY-NUMBER
            self.create_policy_record() # PERFORM 13420-CREATE-POLICY-RECORD
            self.set_beneficiaries() # PERFORM 13430-SET-BENEFICIARIES
            self.send_policy_docs() # PERFORM 13440-SEND-POLICY-DOCS
        else: # ELSE
            self.send_decline_letter() # PERFORM 13450-SEND-DECLINE-LETTER

    def generate_policy_number(self):
        """13410-GENERATE-POLICY-NUMBER."""
        self.ws.WS_DATE-PART = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")  #MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-DATE-PART
        self.ws.WS_TYPE-PART = self.ws.WS_POLICY-TYPE # MOVE WS-POLICY-TYPE TO WS-TYPE-PART
        self.ws.WS_RANDOM-PART = int(random.random() * 99999) # COMPUTE WS-RANDOM-PART = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999
        self.ws.WS_POLICY-NUMBER = f"{self.ws.WS_TYPE-PART}{self.ws.WS_DATE-PART}{self.ws.WS_RANDOM-PART}" # STRING WS-TYPE-PART DELIMITED SIZE WS-DATE-PART DELIMITED SIZE WS-RANDOM-PART DELIMITED SIZE INTO WS-POLICY-NUMBER.

    def create_policy_record(self):
        """13420-CREATE-POLICY-RECORD."""
        policy_record = PolicyRecord() # INITIALIZE WS-POLICY-RECORD
        policy_record.POLICY-REC-NUMBER = self.ws.WS_POLICY-NUMBER # MOVE WS-POLICY-NUMBER TO POLICY-REC-NUMBER
        policy_record.POLICY-REC-TYPE = self.ws.WS_POLICY-TYPE # MOVE WS-POLICY-TYPE TO POLICY-REC-TYPE
        policy_record.POLICY-REC-COVERAGE = self.ws.WS_COVERAGE-AMOUNT # MOVE WS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT TO POLICY-REC-COVERAGE
        policy_record.POLICY-REC-PREMIUM = self.ws.WS_ANNUAL-PREMIUM # MOVE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM TO POLICY-REC-PREMIUM
        policy_record.POLICY-REC-EFF-DATE = self.ws.WS_EFFECTIVE-DATE # MOVE WS-EFFECTIVE-DATE TO POLICY-REC-EFF-DATE
        policy_record.POLICY-REC-EXP-DATE = self.ws.WS_EXPIRATION-DATE # MOVE WS-EXPIRATION-DATE TO POLICY-REC-EXP-DATE
        policy_record.POLICY-REC-STATUS = 'A' # MOVE 'A' TO POLICY-REC-STATUS
        self.storage.write_policy_record(policy_record.__dict__) # WRITE POLICY-RECORD FROM WS-POLICY-RECORD.

    def set_beneficiaries(self):
        """13430-SET-BENEFICIARIES."""
        for self.ws.WS_BENEF-IDX in range(1, 6): # PERFORM VARYING WS-BENEF-IDX FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-BENEF-IDX > 5
            if self.ws.BENEF-NAME and self.ws.WS_BENEF-IDX <= len(self.ws.BENEF-NAME) and self.ws.BENEF-NAME[self.ws.WS_BENEF-IDX-1].strip() != "": # IF BENEF-NAME(WS-BENEF-IDX) NOT = SPACES
                beneficiary_record = BeneficiaryRecord() # INITIALIZE WS-BENEFICIARY-REC
                beneficiary_record.BENEF-REC-POLICY = self.ws.WS_POLICY-NUMBER # MOVE WS-POLICY-NUMBER TO BENEF-REC-POLICY
                beneficiary_record.BENEF-REC-NAME = self.ws.BENEF-NAME[self.ws.WS_BENEF-IDX-1] # MOVE BENEF-NAME(WS-BENEF-IDX) TO BENEF-REC-NAME
                beneficiary_record.BENEF-REC-RELATION = self.ws.BENEF-RELATION[self.ws.WS_BENEF-IDX-1] # MOVE BENEF-REL

```python
from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal
from datetime import datetime


    
    

    
    

    
    
def check_adverse_media(ws_customer_name: str, media_request, media_response) -> int:
    """16116-CHECK-ADVERSE-MEDIA."""
    media_search_name = ws_customer_name # MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO MEDIA-SEARCH-NAME
    media_hits_found = mediasrch(media_request, media_response, media_search_name)  # CALL 'MEDIASRCH' USING MEDIA-REQUEST MEDIA-RESPONSE
    ws_watchlist_hits = 0
    if media_hits_found > 0: # IF MEDIA-HITS-FOUND > 0
        ws_watchlist_hits += media_hits_found  # ADD MEDIA-HITS-FOUND TO WS-WATCHLIST-HITS
    return ws_watchlist_hits

def mediasrch(media_request, media_response, media_search_name: str) -> int:
        # Mock the Mediasrch functionality for demonstration.
        # In a real scenario, this would interact with an external system.
    if media_search_name == 'Test Customer':
        return 3  # Simulate 3 hits for the "Test Customer"
    elif media_search_name == 'High Risk Customer':
        return 10 # Simulate 10 hits for "High Risk Customer"
    else:
        return 0  # Return 0 hits by default

def calculate_match_score(ws_ofac_score: Decimal, ws_pep_score: Decimal, ws_watchlist_hits: int) -> Decimal:
    """16120-CALCULATE-MATCH-SCORE."""
    ws_match_score = Decimal('0')
    if ws_ofac_score > Decimal('0'):  # IF WS-OFAC-SCORE > 0
        ws_match_score += ws_ofac_score  # ADD WS-OFAC-SCORE TO WS-MATCH-SCORE
    if ws_pep_score > Decimal('0'):  # IF WS-PEP-SCORE > 0
        ws_match_score += ws_pep_score  # ADD WS-PEP-SCORE TO WS-MATCH-SCORE
    
    if ws_watchlist_hits > 0:
      ws_match_score = ws_match_score / ws_watchlist_hits
    else:
      ws_match_score = Decimal('0')
    return ws_match_score

def determine_disposition(ws_match_score: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
    """16130-DETERMINE-DISPOSITION."""
    ws_match_type: str = ""
    ws_sar_required: str = ""
    ws_case_status: str = ""

    match ws_match_score: # EVALUATE TRUE
        case score if score >= 90: # WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 90
            ws_match_type = 'CONFIRMED'  # MOVE 'CONFIRMED' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
            ws_sar_required = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-SAR-REQUIRED
        case score if score >= 75: # WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 75
            ws_match_type = 'POTENTIAL'  # MOVE 'POTENTIAL' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
            ws_case_status = 'REVIEW'  # MOVE 'REVIEW' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
        case score if score >= 50: # WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 50
            ws_match_type = 'WEAK'  # MOVE 'WEAK' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
            ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'  # MOVE 'CLEARED' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
        case _: # WHEN OTHER
            ws_match_type = 'FALSE POSITIVE'  # MOVE 'FALSE POSITIVE' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
            ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'  # MOVE 'CLEARED' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
    
    return ws_match_type, ws_sar_required, ws_case_status

def kyc_verification(
    ws_customer_ssn: str,
    ws_customer_dob: str,
    ws_customer_name: str,
    ws_customer_address: str,
    ws_doc_type: str,
    ws_passport_number: str,
    ws_passport_country: str,
    ws_license_number: str,
    ws_license_state: str,
    id_request,
    id_response,
    addr_request,
    addr_response,
    passport_req,
    passport_resp,
    license_req,
    license_resp) -> str:
    """16200-KYC-VERIFICATION."""
    ws_id_status = verify_identity(ws_customer_ssn, ws_customer_dob, ws_customer_name, id_request, id_response)  # PERFORM 16210-VERIFY-IDENTITY
    ws_addr_status = verify_address(ws_customer_address, addr_request, addr_response)  # PERFORM 16220-VERIFY-ADDRESS
    ws_doc_status = verify_documents(ws_doc_type, ws_passport_number, ws_passport_country, ws_license_number, ws_license_state, passport_req, passport_resp, license_req, license_resp)  # PERFORM 16230-VERIFY-DOCUMENTS
    ws_kyc_status = determine_kyc_status(ws_id_status, ws_addr_status, ws_doc_status)  # PERFORM 16240-DETERMINE-KYC-STATUS

    return ws_kyc_status

def verify_identity(ws_customer_ssn: str, ws_customer_dob: str, ws_customer_name: str, id_request, id_response) -> str:
    """16210-VERIFY-IDENTITY."""
    id_verify_ssn = ws_customer_ssn  # MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-SSN TO ID-VERIFY-SSN
    id_verify_dob = ws_customer_dob  # MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-DOB TO ID-VERIFY-DOB
    id_verify_name = ws_customer_name  # MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO ID-VERIFY-NAME
    id_verified = idverify(id_request, id_response, id_verify_ssn, id_verify_dob, id_verify_name) # CALL 'IDVERIFY' USING ID-REQUEST ID-RESPONSE

    if id_verified == 'Y':  # IF ID-VERIFIED = 'Y'
        ws_id_status = 'VERIFIED'  # MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-ID-STATUS
    else:
        ws_id_status = 'FAILED'  # MOVE 'FAILED' TO WS-ID-STATUS
    
    return ws_id_status
    
def idverify(id_request, id_response, id_verify_ssn: str, id_verify_dob: str, id_verify_name: str) -> str:
    if id_verify_name == 'John Doe':
        return 'Y'
    else:
        return 'N'


def verify_address(ws_customer_address: str, addr_request, addr_response) -> str:
    """16220-VERIFY-ADDRESS."""
    addr_verify_input = ws_customer_address  # MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-ADDRESS TO ADDR-VERIFY-INPUT
    addr_verified = addrverify(addr_request, addr_response, addr_verify_input) # CALL 'ADDRVERIFY' USING ADDR-REQUEST ADDR-RESPONSE

    if addr_verified == 'Y':  # IF ADDR-VERIFIED = 'Y'
        ws_addr_status = 'VERIFIED'  # MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-ADDR-STATUS
    else:
        ws_addr_status = 'UNVERIFIED'  # MOVE 'UNVERIFIED' TO WS-ADDR-STATUS
    
    return ws_addr_status

def addrverify(addr_request, addr_response, addr_verify_input: str) -> str:
    if addr_verify_input == '123 Main St':
        return 'Y'
    else:
        return 'N'

def verify_documents(ws_doc_type: str, ws_passport_number: str, ws_passport_country: str, ws_license_number: str, ws_license_state: str, passport_req, passport_resp, license_req, license_resp) -> str:
    """16230-VERIFY-DOCUMENTS."""
    if ws_doc_type == 'PASSPORT':  # IF WS-DOC-TYPE = 'PASSPORT'
        ws_doc_status = verify_passport(ws_passport_number, ws_passport_country, passport_req, passport_resp)  # PERFORM 16232-VERIFY-PASSPORT
    elif ws_doc_type == 'LICENSE':  # ELSE IF WS-DOC-TYPE = 'LICENSE'
        ws_doc_status = verify_license(ws_license_number, ws_license_state, license_req, license_resp)  # PERFORM 16234-VERIFY-LICENSE
    else:
        ws_doc_status = verify_other_doc()  # PERFORM 16236-VERIFY-OTHER-DOC
    
    return ws_doc_status

def verify_passport(ws_passport_number: str, ws_passport_country: str, passport_req, passport_resp) -> str:
    """16232-VERIFY-PASSPORT."""
    passport_verify_num = ws_passport_number  # MOVE WS-PASSPORT-NUMBER TO PASSPORT-VERIFY-NUM
    passport_verify_country = ws_passport_country  # MOVE WS-PASSPORT-COUNTRY TO PASSPORT-VERIFY-COUNTRY
    passport_valid = passverify(passport_req, passport_resp, passport_verify_num, passport_verify_country) # CALL 'PASSVERIFY' USING PASSPORT-REQ PASSPORT-RESP
    if passport_valid == 'Y':  # IF PASSPORT-VALID = 'Y'
        ws_doc_status = 'VERIFIED'  # MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-DOC-STATUS
    else:
        ws_doc_status = 'INVALID'  # MOVE 'INVALID' TO WS-DOC-STATUS
    
    return ws_doc_status

def passverify(passport_req, passport_resp, passport_verify_num: str, passport_verify_country: str) -> str:

        return 'Y'
    else:
        return 'N'

def verify_license(ws_license_number: str, ws_license_state: str, license_req, license_resp) -> str:
    """16234-VERIFY-LICENSE."""
    license_verify_num = ws_license_number  # MOVE WS-LICENSE-NUMBER TO LICENSE-VERIFY-NUM
    license_verify_state = ws_license_state  # MOVE WS-LICENSE-STATE TO LICENSE-VERIFY-STATE
    license_valid = licverify(license_req, license_resp, license_verify_num, license_verify_state) # CALL 'LICVERIFY' USING LICENSE-REQ LICENSE-RESP

    if license_valid == 'Y':  # IF LICENSE-VALID = 'Y'
        ws_doc_status = 'VERIFIED'  # MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-DOC-STATUS
    else:
        ws_doc_status = 'INVALID'  # MOVE 'INVALID' TO WS-DOC-STATUS

    return ws_doc_status

    """16236-VERIFY-OTHER-DOC."""
