    return ws_doc_status

def determine_kyc_status(ws_id_status: str, ws_addr_status: str, ws_doc_status: str) -> str:
    """16240-DETERMINE-KYC-STATUS."""
    if ws_id_status == 'VERIFIED' and \
       ws_addr_status == 'VERIFIED' and \
       ws_doc_status == 'VERIFIED':  # IF WS-ID-STATUS = 'VERIFIED' AND WS-ADDR-STATUS = 'VERIFIED' AND WS-DOC-STATUS = 'VERIFIED'
        ws_kyc_status = 'APPROVED'  # MOVE 'APPROVED' TO WS-KYC-STATUS
    else:
        ws_kyc_status = 'PENDING'  # MOVE 'PENDING' TO WS-KYC-STATUS

    return ws_kyc_status

def sanctions_check(ws_sanctions_hit: str, ws_customer_id: str, ws_account_status: str, ws_freeze_reason: str, escalation_record, account_record) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
    """16300-SANCTIONS-CHECK."""
    if ws_sanctions_hit == 'Y':  # IF WS-SANCTIONS-HIT = 'Y'
        escalation_record, esc_reason, esc_customer, esc_date, esc_priority = escalate_to_compliance(ws_customer_id, escalation_record)  # PERFORM 16310-ESCALATE-TO-COMPLIANCE
        ws_account_status, ws_freeze_reason, account_record = freeze_account(ws_account_status, ws_freeze_reason, account_record)  # PERFORM 16320-FREEZE-ACCOUNT
    
    return ws_account_status, ws_freeze_reason, escalation_record

@dataclass
class EscalationRecord:
    esc_reason: str = ""
    esc_customer: str = ""
    esc_date: str = ""
    esc_priority: str = ""

def escalate_to_compliance(ws_customer_id: str, escalation_record) -> tuple[EscalationRecord, str, str, str, str]:
    """16310-ESCALATE-TO-COMPLIANCE."""
    #INITIALIZE WS-ESCALATION-RECORD
    escalation_record = EscalationRecord()

    esc_reason = 'SANCTIONS HIT'  # MOVE 'SANCTIONS HIT' TO ESC-REASON
    esc_customer = ws_customer_id  # MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-ID TO ESC-CUSTOMER
    esc_date = str(datetime.now().date()) # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO ESC-DATE
    esc_priority = 'URGENT'  # MOVE 'URGENT' TO ESC-PRIORITY
    
    escalation_record.esc_reason = esc_reason
    escalation_record.esc_customer = esc_customer
    escalation_record.esc_date = esc_date
    escalation_record.esc_priority = esc_priority

    #WRITE ESCALATION-RECORD FROM WS-ESCALATION-RECORD.  (Simulated)
    print(f"Writing escalation record: {escalation_record}")
    
    return escalation_record, esc_reason, esc_customer, esc_date, esc_priority

    account_record.status = ws_account_status
    account_record.freeze_reason = ws_freeze_reason

    #REWRITE ACCOUNT-RECORD. (Simulated)

    return ws_account_status, ws_freeze_reason, account_record

def transaction_monitoring(
    ws_daily_trans_count: int,
    ws_velocity_threshold: int,
    ws_daily_trans_amount: Decimal,
    ws_amount_threshold: Decimal,
    ws_round_amount_count: int,
    ws_structuring_detected: str,
    ws_high_risk_country: str,
    ws_new_device: str
) -> tuple[str, str, str, str, str]:
    """16400-TRANSACTION-MONITORING."""
    ws_velocity_flag, ws_amount_flag, ws_fraud_score = check_velocity(ws_daily_trans_count, ws_velocity_threshold, ws_daily_trans_amount, ws_amount_threshold)  # PERFORM 16410-CHECK-VELOCITY
    ws_pattern_flag, ws_fraud_score = check_patterns(ws_round_amount_count, ws_structuring_detected, ws_fraud_score)  # PERFORM 16420-CHECK-PATTERNS
    ws_location_flag, ws_device_flag, ws_fraud_score = check_high_risk(ws_high_risk_country, ws_new_device, ws_fraud_score)  # PERFORM 16430-CHECK-HIGH-RISK
    ws_fraud_decision, ws_manual_review = calculate_risk_score(ws_fraud_score)  # PERFORM 16440-CALCULATE-RISK-SCORE

    return ws_velocity_flag, ws_amount_flag, ws_pattern_flag, ws_location_flag, ws_device_flag, ws_fraud_decision, ws_manual_review

def check_velocity(ws_daily_trans_count: int, ws_velocity_threshold: int, ws_daily_trans_amount: Decimal, ws_amount_threshold: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str, int]:
    """16410-CHECK-VELOCITY."""
    ws_velocity_flag: str = ""
    ws_amount_flag: str = ""
    ws_fraud_score: int = 0

    if ws_daily_trans_count > ws_velocity_threshold:  # IF WS-DAILY-TRANS-COUNT > WS-VELOCITY-THRESHOLD
        ws_velocity_flag = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-VELOCITY-FLAG
        ws_fraud_score += 20  # ADD 20 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
    if ws_daily_trans_amount > ws_amount_threshold:  # IF WS-DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT > WS-AMOUNT-THRESHOLD
        ws_amount_flag = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-AMOUNT-FLAG
        ws_fraud_score += 20  # ADD 20 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
    
    return ws_velocity_flag, ws_amount_flag, ws_fraud_score

def check_patterns(ws_round_amount_count: int, ws_structuring_detected: str, ws_fraud_score: int) -> tuple[str, int]:
    """16420-CHECK-PATTERNS."""
    ws_pattern_flag: str = ""
    if ws_round_amount_count > 5:  # IF WS-ROUND-AMOUNT-COUNT > 5
        ws_pattern_flag = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PATTERN-FLAG
        ws_fraud_score += 15  # ADD 15 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
    if ws_structuring_detected == 'Y':  # IF WS-STRUCTURING-DETECTED = 'Y'
        ws_pattern_flag = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PATTERN-FLAG
        ws_fraud_score += 30  # ADD 30 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE

    return ws_pattern_flag, ws_fraud_score

def check_high_risk(ws_high_risk_country: str, ws_new_device: str, ws_fraud_score: int) -> tuple[str, str, int]:
    """16430-CHECK-HIGH-RISK."""
    ws_location_flag: str = ""
    ws_device_flag: str = ""
    if ws_high_risk_country == 'Y':  # IF WS-HIGH-RISK-COUNTRY = 'Y'
        ws_location_flag = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-LOCATION-FLAG
        ws_fraud_score += 25  # ADD 25 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
    if ws_new_device == 'Y':  # IF WS-NEW-DEVICE = 'Y'
        ws_device_flag = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-DEVICE-FLAG
        ws_fraud_score += 10  # ADD 10 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
    
    return ws_location_flag, ws_device_flag, ws_fraud_score

def calculate_risk_score(ws_fraud_score: int) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """16440-CALCULATE-RISK-SCORE."""
    ws_fraud_decision: str = ""
    ws_manual_review: str = ""

    match ws_fraud_score: # EVALUATE TRUE
        case score if score >= 80: # WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 80
            ws_fraud_decision = 'BLOCK'  # MOVE 'BLOCK' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
            ws_manual_review = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-MANUAL-REVIEW
        case score if score >= 60: # WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 60
            ws_fraud_decision = 'REVIEW'  # MOVE 'REVIEW' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
            ws_manual_review = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-MANUAL-REVIEW
        case score if score >= 40: # WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 40
            ws_fraud_decision = 'MONITOR'  # MOVE 'MONITOR' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
        case _: # WHEN OTHER
            ws_fraud_decision = 'APPROVE'  # MOVE 'APPROVE' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
    
    return ws_fraud_decision, ws_manual_review

def suspicious_activity_report(
    ws_sar_required: str,
    ws_customer_name: str,
    ws_customer_address: str,
    ws_customer_ssn: str,
    ws_transaction_amount: Decimal
) -> None:
    """16500-SUSPICIOUS-ACTIVITY-REPORT."""
    if ws_sar_required == 'Y':  # IF WS-SAR-REQUIRED = 'Y'
        sar_subject_name, sar_subject_addr, sar_subject_ssn, sar_amount, sar_activity_date = gather_sar_data(ws_customer_name, ws_customer_address, ws_customer_ssn, ws_transaction_amount)  # PERFORM 16510-GATHER-SAR-DATA
        ws_sar_record = generate_sar(sar_subject_name, sar_subject_addr, sar_amount, sar_activity_date)  # PERFORM 16520-GENERATE-SAR
        file_sar(ws_sar_record)  # PERFORM 16530-FILE-SAR

def gather_sar_data(ws_customer_name: str, ws_customer_address: str, ws_customer_ssn: str, ws_transaction_amount: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str, str, Decimal, str]:
    """16510-GATHER-SAR-DATA."""
    sar_subject_name = ws_customer_name  # MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO SAR-SUBJECT-NAME
    sar_subject_addr = ws_customer_address  # MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-ADDRESS TO SAR-SUBJECT-ADDR
    sar_subject_ssn = ws_customer_ssn  # MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-SSN TO SAR-SUBJECT-SSN
    sar_amount = ws_transaction_amount  # MOVE WS-TRANSACTION-AMOUNT TO SAR-AMOUNT
    sar_activity_date = str(datetime.now().date())  # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO SAR-ACTIVITY-DATE

    return sar_subject_name, sar_subject_addr, sar_subject_ssn, sar_amount, sar_activity_date

@dataclass
class SarRecord:
    sar_rec_name: str = ""
    sar_rec_addr: str = ""
    sar_rec_amount: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    sar_rec_date: str = ""
    sar_rec_narrative: str = ""

def generate_sar(sar_subject_name: str, sar_subject_addr: str, sar_amount: Decimal, sar_activity_date: str) -> SarRecord:
    """16520-GENERATE-SAR."""
    #INITIALIZE WS-SAR-RECORD
    ws_sar_record = SarRecord()

    ws_sar_record.sar_rec_name = sar_subject_name  # MOVE SAR-SUBJECT-NAME TO SAR-REC-NAME
    ws_sar_record.sar_rec_addr = sar_subject_addr  # MOVE SAR-SUBJECT-ADDR TO SAR-REC-ADDR
    ws_sar_record.sar_rec_amount = sar_amount  # MOVE SAR-AMOUNT TO SAR-REC-AMOUNT
    ws_sar_record.sar_rec_date = sar_activity_date  # MOVE SAR-ACTIVITY-DATE TO SAR-REC-DATE
    ws_sar_record.sar_rec_narrative = 'SUSPICIOUS PATTERN DETECTED'  # MOVE 'SUSPICIOUS PATTERN DETECTED' TO SAR-REC-NARRATIVE

    return ws_sar_record

def file_sar(ws_sar_record: SarRecord) -> None:
    """16530-FILE-SAR."""
    sar_status = 'PENDING'  # MOVE 'PENDING' TO SAR-STATUS

    #WRITE SAR-RECORD FROM WS-SAR-RECORD.  (Simulated)
    print(f"Writing SAR record: {ws_sar_record} with status {sar_status}")

def customer_service(ws_open_date: str, ws_case_type: str, ws_customer_account, ws_customer_id: str, ws_channel: str, history_file, case_file, ws_billing_error: str, ws_credit_amount: Decimal, ws_fraud_case: str, ws_follow_up_required: str, ws_customer_phone: str) -> None:
    """17000-CUSTOMER-SERVICE."""
    ws_case_id, ws_case_status, ws_case_priority, ws_target_date = create_case(ws_open_date, ws_case_type)  # PERFORM 17100-CREATE-CASE
    ws_queue, ws_assigned_agent = route_case(ws_case_type)  # PERFORM 17200-ROUTE-CASE
    
    ws_interaction_count, ws_account_history, ws_previous_case_count, ws_caller_type, ws_resolution_code = process_case(ws_customer_account, ws_customer_id, ws_channel, ws_assigned_agent, history_file, case_file, ws_billing_error, ws_credit_amount, ws_fraud_case)  # PERFORM 17300-PROCESS-CASE
    resolve_case(ws_case_id, ws_resolution_code)  # PERFORM 17400-RESOLVE-CASE
    follow_up(ws_case_id, ws_follow_up_required, ws_close_date, ws_customer_phone)  # PERFORM 17500-FOLLOW-UP

def create_case(ws_open_date: str, ws_case_type: str) -> tuple[str, str, int, int]:
    """17100-CREATE-CASE."""
    ws_case_id = generate_case_id()  # PERFORM 17110-GENERATE-CASE-ID
    #MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-OPEN-DATE  (Assuming ws_open_date is passed in)
    ws_case_status = 'OPEN'  # MOVE 'OPEN' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
    ws_case_priority, ws_target_date = categorize_case(ws_open_date, ws_case_type)  # PERFORM 17120-CATEGORIZE-CASE

    return ws_case_id, ws_case_status, ws_case_priority, ws_target_date

def generate_case_id() -> str:
    """17110-GENERATE-CASE-ID."""
    ws_date_part = str(datetime.now().date()).replace('-', '') # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-DATE-PART
    ws_random_part = int(random() * 99999)  # COMPUTE WS-RANDOM-PART = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999
    ws_case_id = f"CS{ws_date_part}{ws_random_part}"
    return ws_case_id

from random import random

def categorize_case(ws_open_date: str, ws_case_type: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """17120-CATEGORIZE-CASE."""
    match ws_case_type: # EVALUATE WS-CASE-TYPE
        case 'BILLING INQUIRY': # WHEN 'BILLING INQUIRY'
            ws_case_priority = 2  # MOVE 2 TO WS-CASE-PRIORITY
        case 'FRAUD REPORT': # WHEN 'FRAUD REPORT'
            ws_case_priority = 1  # MOVE 1 TO WS-CASE-PRIORITY
        case 'ACCOUNT ACCESS': # WHEN 'ACCOUNT ACCESS'
            ws_case_priority = 1  # MOVE 1 TO WS-CASE-PRIORITY
        case 'GENERAL INQUIRY': # WHEN 'GENERAL INQUIRY'
            ws_case_priority = 3  # MOVE 3 TO WS-CASE-PRIORITY
        case _: # WHEN OTHER
            ws_case_priority = 3  # MOVE 3 TO WS-CASE-PRIORITY
    
    open_date_dt = datetime.strptime(ws_open_date, '%Y-%m-%d').date() if isinstance(ws_open_date, str) else ws_open_date

    ws_target_date = open_date_dt.toordinal() + (ws_case_priority * 2)  # COMPUTE WS-TARGET-DATE = FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-OPEN-DATE) + WS-CASE-PRIORITY * 2
    return ws_case_priority, ws_target_date

def route_case(ws_case_type: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """17200-ROUTE-CASE."""
    match ws_case_type: # EVALUATE WS-CASE-TYPE
        case 'BILLING INQUIRY': # WHEN 'BILLING INQUIRY'
            ws_queue = 'BILLING'  # MOVE 'BILLING' TO WS-QUEUE
        case 'FRAUD REPORT': # WHEN 'FRAUD REPORT'
            ws_queue = 'FRAUD'  # MOVE 'FRAUD' TO WS-QUEUE
        case 'ACCOUNT ACCESS': # WHEN 'ACCOUNT ACCESS'
            ws_queue = 'SECURITY'  # MOVE 'SECURITY' TO WS-QUEUE
        case 'LOAN INQUIRY': # WHEN 'LOAN INQUIRY'
            ws_queue = 'LENDING'  # MOVE 'LENDING' TO WS-QUEUE
        case _: # WHEN OTHER
            ws_queue = 'GENERAL'  # MOVE 'GENERAL' TO WS-QUEUE
    
    ws_assigned_agent = assign_agent(ws_queue) # PERFORM 17210-ASSIGN-AGENT
    return ws_queue, ws_assigned_agent

def assign_agent(ws_queue: str) -> str:
    """17210-ASSIGN-AGENT."""
    ws_assigned_agent = routecase(ws_queue)  # CALL 'ROUTECASE' USING WS-QUEUE WS-ASSIGNED-AGENT
    if ws_assigned_agent == '':  # IF WS-ASSIGNED-AGENT = SPACES
        ws_case_status = 'UNASSIGNED'  # MOVE 'UNASSIGNED' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
    else:
        ws_case_status = 'ASSIGNED'  # MOVE 'ASSIGNED' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
    
    return ws_assigned_agent

def routecase(ws_queue: str) -> str:
    if ws_queue == 'FRAUD':
        return "Agent123"
    else:
        return ""

def process_case(ws_customer_account: str, ws_customer_id: str, ws_channel: str, ws_assigned_agent: str, history_file, case_file, ws_billing_error: str, ws_credit_amount: Decimal, ws_fraud_case: str) -> tuple[int, str, int, str, str]:
    """17300-PROCESS-CASE."""
    ws_interaction_count = log_interaction(ws_channel, ws_assigned_agent)  # PERFORM 17310-LOG-INTERACTION
    ws_account_history, ws_previous_case_count, ws_caller_type = research_issue(ws_customer_account, ws_customer_id, history_file, case_file)  # PERFORM 17320-RESEARCH-ISSUE
    ws_resolution_code = determine_resolution(ws_billing_error, ws_credit_amount, ws_fraud_case)  # PERFORM 17330-DETERMINE-RESOLUTION
    
    return ws_interaction_count, ws_account_history, ws_previous_case_count, ws_caller_type, ws_resolution_code

INT_DATE = {}
INT_TIME = {}
INT_CHANNEL = {}
INT_AGENT = {}

def log_interaction(ws_channel: str, ws_assigned_agent: str) -> int:
    """17310-LOG-INTERACTION."""
    global INT_DATE, INT_TIME, INT_CHANNEL, INT_AGENT

    ws_interaction_count = len(INT_DATE) + 1
    
    INT_DATE[ws_interaction_count] = str(datetime.now().date())
    INT_TIME[ws_interaction_count] = str

# === CHUNK 10 (lines 6013-6680) ===
```python
from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Optional, List
import datetime
import random

# Dummy Functions (replace with actual implementations)
def INTEGER_OF_DATE(date: datetime.date) -> int:
    """COBOL: FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE"""
    return date.toordinal()

def RANDOM() -> float:
    """COBOL: FUNCTION RANDOM"""
    return random.random()

def MOD(dividend: int, divisor: int) -> int:
    """COBOL: FUNCTION MOD"""
    return dividend % divisor

@dataclass

# File structures (assuming these are lists of records)
TRANSACTION_FILE: List[TransactionRecord] = []
CUSTOMER_FILE: List[CustomerRecord] = []
PERF_LOG_FILE: List[PerformanceLogRecord] = []
DAILY_SUMMARY_FILE: List[DailySummaryRecord] = []
ACCOUNT_FILE: List[AccountRecord] = []
CSV_EXPORT_FILE: List[str] = [] # Placeholder for CSV file data
XML_EXPORT_FILE: List[str] = [] # Placeholder for XML file data
JSON_EXPORT_FILE: List[str] = [] # Placeholder for JSON file data

ARCHIVE_FILE: List[ArchiveRecord] = []
CARD_FILE: List[CardRecord] = []

# Global instance of working storage
ws = WSRecords()

def update_next_run_date(run_frequency: str):
    """COBOL: EVALUATE run_frequency..."""
    match run_frequency:
        case 'DAILY':
            ws.WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = datetime.date.fromordinal(INTEGER_OF_DATE(ws.WS_LAST_RUN_DATE) + 1)
        case 'WEEKLY':
            ws.WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = datetime.date.fromordinal(INTEGER_OF_DATE(ws.WS_LAST_RUN_DATE) + 7)
        case 'MONTHLY':
            ws.WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = datetime.date.fromordinal(INTEGER_OF_DATE(ws.WS_LAST_RUN_DATE) + 30)
        case 'QUARTERLY':
            ws.WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = datetime.date.fromordinal(INTEGER_OF_DATE(ws.WS_LAST_RUN_DATE) + 90)
        case 'YEARLY':
            ws.WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = datetime.date.fromordinal(INTEGER_OF_DATE(ws.WS_LAST_RUN_DATE) + 365)

def data_analytics():
    """
    *----------------------------------------------------------------*
    * DATA ANALYTICS AND REPORTING PROCEDURES                       *
    *----------------------------------------------------------------*
    """
    collect_metrics() # 21100-COLLECT-METRICS
    aggregate_data() # 21200-AGGREGATE-DATA
    calculate_kpi() # 21300-CALCULATE-KPI
    generate_dashboard() # 21400-GENERATE-DASHBOARD
    export_data() # 21500-EXPORT-DATA

def collect_metrics():
    """21100-COLLECT-METRICS."""
    collect_transaction_metrics() # 21110-COLLECT-TRANSACTION-METRICS
    collect_customer_metrics() # 21120-COLLECT-CUSTOMER-METRICS
    collect_performance_metrics() # 21130-COLLECT-PERFORMANCE-METRICS

def collect_transaction_metrics():
    """21110-COLLECT-TRANSACTION-METRICS."""
    ws.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal('0.00')  # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT
    ws.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT = 0  # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
    ws.WS_AVG_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal('0.00')  # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-AVG-TRANS-AMOUNT
    
    ws.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while ws.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y': # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
        try:
            ws_trans_rec = TRANSACTION_FILE.pop(0)  # READ TRANSACTION-FILE INTO WS-TRANS-REC
            ws.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
            ws.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT += ws_trans_rec.TRANS_AMOUNT  # ADD TRANS-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT
        except IndexError:
            ws.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            
    if ws.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT > 0:  # IF WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT > 0
        ws.WS_AVG_TRANS_AMOUNT = ws.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT / ws.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT  # COMPUTE WS-AVG-TRANS-AMOUNT = WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT / WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
    
    ws.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
    TRANSACTION_FILE.clear()

def collect_customer_metrics():
    """21120-COLLECT-CUSTOMER-METRICS."""
    ws.WS_ACTIVE_CUSTOMERS = 0  # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS
    ws.WS_NEW_CUSTOMERS = 0  # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS
    ws.WS_CHURNED_CUSTOMERS = 0  # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS
    
    ws.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while ws.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
        try:
            ws_cust_rec = CUSTOMER_FILE.pop(0)  # READ CUSTOMER-FILE INTO WS-CUST-REC
            if ws_cust_rec.CUST_STATUS == 'A':  # IF CUST-STATUS = 'A'
                ws.WS_ACTIVE_CUSTOMERS += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS
            if ws_cust_rec.CUST_OPEN_DATE and ws_cust_rec.CUST_OPEN_DATE >= ws.WS_PROCESS_DATE:  # IF CUST-OPEN-DATE >= WS-PERIOD-START
                ws.WS_NEW_CUSTOMERS += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS
            if ws_cust_rec.CUST_CLOSE_DATE and ws_cust_rec.CUST_CLOSE_DATE >= ws.WS_PROCESS_DATE:  # IF CUST-CLOSE-DATE >= WS-PERIOD-START
                ws.WS_CHURNED_CUSTOMERS += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS
        except IndexError:
            ws.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            
    ws.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
    CUSTOMER_FILE.clear()

def collect_performance_metrics():
    """21130-COLLECT-PERFORMANCE-METRICS."""
    ws.WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL = Decimal('0.00')  # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL
    ws.WS_RESPONSE_COUNT = 0  # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RESPONSE-COUNT
    
    ws.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while ws.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
        try:
            ws_perf_rec = PERF_LOG_FILE.pop(0)  # READ PERF-LOG-FILE INTO WS-PERF-REC
            ws.WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL += ws_perf_rec.PERF_RESPONSE_TIME  # ADD PERF-RESPONSE-TIME TO WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL
            ws.WS_RESPONSE_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-RESPONSE-COUNT
        except IndexError:
            ws.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            
    if ws.WS_RESPONSE_COUNT > 0:  # IF WS-RESPONSE-COUNT > 0
        ws.WS_AVG_RESPONSE_TIME = ws.WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL / ws.WS_RESPONSE_COUNT  # COMPUTE WS-AVG-RESPONSE-TIME = WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL / WS-RESPONSE-COUNT
    
    ws.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
    PERF_LOG_FILE.clear()

def aggregate_data():
    """21200-AGGREGATE-DATA."""
    daily_aggregation() # 21210-DAILY-AGGREGATION
    weekly_aggregation() # 21220-WEEKLY-AGGREGATION
    monthly_aggregation() # 21230-MONTHLY-AGGREGATION

def daily_aggregation():
    """21210-DAILY-AGGREGATION."""
    ws.WS_DAILY_SUMMARY = DailySummaryRecord()  # INITIALIZE WS-DAILY-SUMMARY
    ws.WS_DAILY_SUMMARY.DAILY_DATE = ws.WS_PROCESS_DATE  # MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO DAILY-DATE
    ws.WS_DAILY_SUMMARY.DAILY_TRANS_COUNT = ws.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT  # MOVE WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT TO DAILY-TRANS-COUNT
    ws.WS_DAILY_SUMMARY.DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT = ws.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT  # MOVE WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT TO DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT
    ws.WS_DAILY_SUMMARY.DAILY_DEPOSITS = ws.WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS  # MOVE WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS TO DAILY-DEPOSITS
    ws.WS_DAILY_SUMMARY.DAILY_WITHDRAWALS = ws.WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS  # MOVE WS-TOTAL-WITHDRAWALS TO DAILY-WITHDRAWALS
    DAILY_SUMMARY_FILE.append(ws.WS_DAILY_SUMMARY)  # WRITE DAILY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-DAILY-SUMMARY

def weekly_aggregation():
    """21220-WEEKLY-AGGREGATION."""
    if ws.WS_DAY_OF_WEEK == 7:  # IF WS-DAY-OF-WEEK = 7
        ws.WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY = WeeklySummaryRecord()  # INITIALIZE WS-WEEKLY-SUMMARY
        ws.WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY.WEEKLY_WEEK = ws.WS_WEEK_NUMBER  # MOVE WS-WEEK-NUMBER TO WEEKLY-WEEK
        sum_week_data() # 21225-SUM-WEEK-DATA
        # Assuming WEEKLY_SUMMARY_RECORD is created and handled elsewhere
        # For example:
        # WEEKLY_SUMMARY_FILE.append(ws.WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY) #  WRITE WEEKLY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-WEEKLY-SUMMARY

def sum_week_data():
    """21225-SUM-WEEK-DATA."""
    weekly_trans_count = 0  # MOVE ZEROES TO WEEKLY-TRANS-COUNT
    weekly_trans_amount = Decimal('0.00')  # MOVE ZEROES TO WEEKLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
    for _ in range(7):  # PERFORM 7 TIMES
        if DAILY_SUMMARY_FILE:
            daily_summary = DAILY_SUMMARY_FILE.pop(0)
            weekly_trans_count += daily_summary.DAILY_TRANS_COUNT  # ADD DAILY-TRANS-COUNT TO WEEKLY-TRANS-COUNT
            weekly_trans_amount += daily_summary.DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT  # ADD DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT TO WEEKLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
        else:
            break

    if ws.WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY:
      ws.WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY.WEEKLY_TRANS_COUNT = weekly_trans_count
      ws.WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY.WEEKLY_TRANS_AMOUNT = weekly_trans_amount
    DAILY_SUMMARY_FILE.clear()

def monthly_aggregation():
    """21230-MONTHLY-AGGREGATION."""
    if ws.WS_END_OF_MONTH == 'Y':  # IF WS-END-OF-MONTH = 'Y'
        ws.WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY = MonthlySummaryRecord()  # INITIALIZE WS-MONTHLY-SUMMARY
        ws.WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.MONTHLY_MONTH = ws.WS_CURR_MONTH  # MOVE WS-CURR-MONTH TO MONTHLY-MONTH
        ws.WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.MONTHLY_YEAR = ws.WS_CURR_YEAR  # MOVE WS-CURR-YEAR TO MONTHLY-YEAR
        sum_month_data() # 21235-SUM-MONTH-DATA
        # Assuming MONTHLY_SUMMARY_RECORD is created and handled elsewhere
        # For example:
        # MONTHLY_SUMMARY_FILE.append(ws.WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY)  # WRITE MONTHLY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-MONTHLY-SUMMARY

def sum_month_data():
    """21235-SUM-MONTH-DATA."""
    monthly_trans_count = 0  # MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-TRANS-COUNT
    monthly_trans_amount = Decimal('0.00')  # MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
    monthly_new_accounts = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-NEW-ACCOUNTS
    monthly_closed_accounts = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-CLOSED-ACCOUNTS

    ws.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while ws.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y': # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
        try:
            ws.WS_DAILY_SUM_REC = DAILY_SUMMARY_FILE.pop(0)  # READ DAILY-SUMMARY-FILE INTO WS-DAILY-SUM-REC
            if ws.WS_DAILY_SUM_REC.DAILY_MONTH == ws.WS_CURR_MONTH:  # IF DAILY-MONTH = WS-CURR-MONTH
                monthly_trans_count += ws.WS_DAILY_SUM_REC.DAILY_TRANS_COUNT  # ADD DAILY-TRANS-COUNT TO MONTHLY-TRANS-COUNT
                monthly_trans_amount += ws.WS_DAILY_SUM_REC.DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT  # ADD DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT TO MONTHLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
        except IndexError:
            ws.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

    ws.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

    if ws.WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY:
      ws.WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.MONTHLY_TRANS_COUNT = monthly_trans_count
      ws.WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.MONTHLY_TRANS_AMOUNT = monthly_trans_amount
      ws.WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.MONTHLY_NEW_ACCOUNTS = monthly_new_accounts
      ws.WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.MONTHLY_CLOSED_ACCOUNTS = monthly_closed_accounts

    DAILY_SUMMARY_FILE.clear()

def calculate_kpi():
    """21300-CALCULATE-KPI."""
    calc_financial_kpi() # 21310-CALC-FINANCIAL-KPI
    calc_operational_kpi() # 21320-CALC-OPERATIONAL-KPI
    calc_customer_kpi() # 21330-CALC-CUSTOMER-KPI

def calc_financial_kpi():
    """21310-CALC-FINANCIAL-KPI."""
    if ws.WS_TOTAL_ASSETS > 0:  # IF WS-TOTAL-ASSETS > 0
        ws.WS_ROA = (ws.WS_NET_INCOME / ws.WS_TOTAL_ASSETS) * 100  # COMPUTE WS-ROA = (WS-NET-INCOME / WS-TOTAL-ASSETS) * 100
    if ws.WS_TOTAL_EQUITY > 0:  # IF WS-TOTAL-EQUITY > 0
        ws.WS_ROE = (ws.WS_NET_INCOME / ws.WS_TOTAL_EQUITY) * 100  # COMPUTE WS-ROE = (WS-NET-INCOME / WS-TOTAL-EQUITY) * 100
    if ws.WS_INTEREST_EXPENSE > 0:  # IF WS-INTEREST-EXPENSE > 0
        ws.WS_NIM = ((ws.WS_INTEREST_INCOME - ws.WS_INTEREST_EXPENSE) / ws.WS_EARNING_ASSETS) * 100  # COMPUTE WS-NIM = ((WS-INTEREST-INCOME - WS-INTEREST-EXPENSE) / WS-EARNING-ASSETS) * 100

def calc_operational_kpi():
    """21320-CALC-OPERATIONAL-KPI."""
    if ws.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT > 0:  # IF WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT > 0
        ws.WS_ERROR_RATE = (ws.WS_ERROR_COUNT / ws.WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT) * 100  # COMPUTE WS-ERROR-RATE = (WS-ERROR-COUNT / WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT) * 100
    ws.WS_SLA_COMPLIANCE = (ws.WS_WITHIN_SLA_COUNT / ws.WS_TOTAL_CASES) * 100  # COMPUTE WS-SLA-COMPLIANCE = (WS-WITHIN-SLA-COUNT / WS-TOTAL-CASES) * 100
    ws.WS_FIRST_CALL_RESOLUTION = (ws.WS_FCR_COUNT / ws.WS_TOTAL_CALLS) * 100  # COMPUTE WS-FIRST-CALL-RESOLUTION = (WS-FCR-COUNT / WS-TOTAL-CALLS) * 100

def calc_customer_kpi():
    """21330-CALC-CUSTOMER-KPI."""
    if ws.WS_ACTIVE_CUSTOMERS > 0:  # IF WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS > 0
        ws.WS_CHURN_RATE = (ws.WS_CHURNED_CUSTOMERS / ws.WS_ACTIVE_CUSTOMERS) * 100  # COMPUTE WS-CHURN-RATE = (WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS / WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS) * 100
    ws.WS_ACQUISITION_COST = ws.WS_MARKETING_SPEND / ws.WS_NEW_CUSTOMERS  # COMPUTE WS-ACQUISITION-COST = WS-MARKETING-SPEND / WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS
    ws.WS_LIFETIME_VALUE = ws.WS_AVG_REVENUE_PER_CUSTOMER * ws.WS_AVG_CUSTOMER_TENURE  # COMPUTE WS-LIFETIME-VALUE = WS-AVG-REVENUE-PER-CUSTOMER * WS-AVG-CUSTOMER-TENURE

def generate_dashboard():
    """21400-GENERATE-DASHBOARD."""
    create_executive_dashboard() # 21410-CREATE-EXECUTIVE-DASHBOARD
    create_operations_dashboard() # 21420-CREATE-OPERATIONS-DASHBOARD
    create_risk_dashboard() # 21430-CREATE-RISK-DASHBOARD

    def perform_shipping(self):
        """
        MOVE 'EXPRESS' TO SHIP-METHOD
        COMPUTE SHIP-EST-DELIVERY = FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-PROCESS-DATE) + 2
        ELSE
        MOVE 'STANDARD' TO SHIP-METHOD
        COMPUTE SHIP-EST-DELIVERY = FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-PROCESS-DATE) + 7
        END-IF
        WRITE SHIPMENT-RECORD FROM WS-SHIPMENT-RECORD.
        """
        if self.WS_PROCESS_DATE == '20240101': #example condition
            self.SHIP_METHOD = 'EXPRESS'
            self.SHIP_EST_DELIVERY = self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 2
        else:
            self.SHIP_METHOD = 'STANDARD'
            self.SHIP_EST_DELIVERY = self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 7
        self.SHIPMENT_RECORD.append(self.WS_SHIPMENT_RECORD) #placeholder

    def integer_of_date(self, date_str: str) -> int:
        """COBOL FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE"""
        year = int(date_str[:4])
        month = int(date_str[4:6])
        day = int(date_str[6:8])
        date_object = datetime.date(year, month, day)
        return date_object.toordinal()

    def card_blocking(self):
        """
        23500-CARD-BLOCKING.
        MOVE 'B' TO CARD-STATUS
        MOVE WS-BLOCK-REASON TO CARD-BLOCK-REASON
        MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO CARD-BLOCK-DATE
        REWRITE CARD-RECORD FROM WS-CARD-RECORD
        MOVE 'CARD-BLOCKED' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
        MOVE 'SMS' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
        STRING 'Your card has been blocked: ' DELIMITED SIZE
                WS-BLOCK-REASON DELIMITED SIZE
            INTO WS-NOTIF-BODY
        PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION.
        """
        self.CARD_STATUS = 'B'
        self.CARD_BLOCK_REASON = "Reason" #Replace with actual value
        self.CARD_BLOCK_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.rewrite_card_record()
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'CARD-BLOCKED'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'SMS'
        self.WS_NOTIF_BODY = f'Your card has been blocked: {self.CARD_BLOCK_REASON}'
        self.send_notification()

    def wire_transfer(self):
        """
        24000-WIRE-TRANSFER.
        PERFORM 24100-VALIDATE-WIRE-REQUEST
        IF WS-WIRE-VALID = 'Y'
            PERFORM 24200-OFAC-SCREENING
            IF WS-OFAC-CLEAR = 'Y'
                PERFORM 24300-PROCESS-WIRE
                PERFORM 24400-SEND-CONFIRMATION
            ELSE
                PERFORM 24500-REJECT-WIRE
            END-IF
        END-IF.
        """
        self.validate_wire_request()
        if self.WS_WIRE_VALID == 'Y':
            self.ofac_screening()
            if self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR == 'Y':
                self.process_wire()
                self.send_confirmation()
            else:
                self.reject_wire()

    def validate_wire_request(self):
        """
        24100-VALIDATE-WIRE-REQUEST.
        MOVE 'Y' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
        IF WS-WIRE-AMOUNT <= 0
            MOVE 'N' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
            MOVE 'INVALID AMOUNT' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
        END-IF
        IF WS-WIRE-AMOUNT > WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
            MOVE 'N' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
            MOVE 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
        END-IF
        IF WS-BENEFICIARY-ACCOUNT = SPACES
            MOVE 'N' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
            MOVE 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
        END-IF
        IF WS-WIRE-AMOUNT > 10000
            MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CTR-REQUIRED
        END-IF.
        """
        self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'Y'
        if self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT <= 0:
            self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
        if self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
            self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
        if self.WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT == "":
            self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED'
        if self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > 10000:
            self.WS_CTR_REQUIRED = 'Y'

    def ofac_screening(self):
        """
        24200-OFAC-SCREENING.
        MOVE 'Y' TO WS-OFAC-CLEAR
        MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-NAME TO OFAC-SEARCH-NAME
        CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING OFAC-REQUEST OFAC-RESPONSE
        IF OFAC-MATCH-FOUND = 'Y'
            IF OFAC-MATCH-SCORE >= 85
                MOVE 'N' TO WS-OFAC-CLEAR
                MOVE 'OFAC MATCH' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
            END-IF
        END-IF
        MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-BANK TO OFAC-SEARCH-BANK
        CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING OFAC-REQUEST OFAC-RESPONSE
        IF OFAC-MATCH-FOUND = 'Y'
            IF OFAC-MATCH-SCORE >= 85
                MOVE 'N' TO WS-OFAC-CLEAR
                MOVE 'BANK OFAC MATCH' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
            END-IF
        END-IF.
        """
        self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'Y'
        self.OFAC_SEARCH_NAME = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME
        self.call_ofacsrch()
        if self.OFAC_RESPONSE.ofac_match_found == 'Y':
            if self.OFAC_RESPONSE.ofac_match_score >= 85:
                self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
                self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'OFAC MATCH'
        self.OFAC_SEARCH_BANK = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK
        self.call_ofacsrch()
        if self.OFAC_RESPONSE.ofac_match_found == 'Y':
            if self.OFAC_RESPONSE.ofac_match_score >= 85:
                self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
                self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BANK OFAC MATCH'

    def call_ofacsrch(self):
        """CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING OFAC-REQUEST OFAC-RESPONSE"""
        # Placeholder for OFAC search logic.  This would normally involve calling an external API or function.
        # Example:
        # self.OFAC_RESPONSE = ofac_search(self.OFAC_REQUEST)
        # For now, we'll simulate a match with a 90 score 50% of the time.
        import random
        if random.random() < 0.5:
            self.OFAC_RESPONSE.ofac_match_found = 'Y'
            self.OFAC_RESPONSE.ofac_match_score = 90
        else:
            self.OFAC_RESPONSE.ofac_match_found = 'N'
            self.OFAC_RESPONSE.ofac_match_score = 0


    def process_wire(self):
        """
        24300-PROCESS-WIRE.
        PERFORM 24310-DEBIT-ORIGINATOR
        PERFORM 24320-CREATE-WIRE-MESSAGE
        PERFORM 24330-TRANSMIT-WIRE
        PERFORM 24340-RECORD-WIRE.
        """
        self.debit_originator()
        self.create_wire_message()
        self.transmit_wire()
        self.record_wire()

    def debit_originator(self):
        """
        24310-DEBIT-ORIGINATOR.
        SUBTRACT WS-WIRE-AMOUNT FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        SUBTRACT WS-WIRE-FEE FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT.
        """
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_WIRE_FEE
        self.update_account()

    def update_account(self):
        """PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT"""
        pass # Placeholder for account update logic

    def create_wire_message(self):
        """
        24320-CREATE-WIRE-MESSAGE.
        INITIALIZE WS-SWIFT-MESSAGE
        MOVE 'MT103' TO SWIFT-MSG-TYPE
        MOVE WS-WIRE-REF TO SWIFT-TXN-REF
        MOVE WS-WIRE-DATE TO SWIFT-VALUE-DATE
        MOVE WS-WIRE-CURRENCY TO SWIFT-CURRENCY
        MOVE WS-WIRE-AMOUNT TO SWIFT-AMOUNT
        MOVE WS-ORIGINATOR-NAME TO SWIFT-ORDERING-CUST
        MOVE WS-ORIGINATOR-ACCOUNT TO SWIFT-ORDERING-ACCT
        MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-NAME TO SWIFT-BENEF-CUST
        MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-ACCOUNT TO SWIFT-BENEF-ACCT
        MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-BANK-BIC TO SWIFT-BENEF-BANK
        MOVE WS-PURPOSE TO SWIFT-REMIT-INFO.
        """
        self.WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE = WSSwiftMessage()  # Initialize (replace with actual initialization logic if needed)
        self.SWIFT_MSG_TYPE = 'MT103'
        self.SWIFT_TXN_REF = self.WS_WIRE_REF
        self.SWIFT_VALUE_DATE = self.WS_WIRE_DATE
        self.SWIFT_CURRENCY = self.WS_WIRE_CURRENCY
        self.SWIFT_AMOUNT = self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
        self.SWIFT_ORDERING_CUST = self.WS_ORIGINATOR_NAME
        self.SWIFT_ORDERING_ACCT = self.WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT
        self.SWIFT_BENEF_CUST = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME
        self.SWIFT_BENEF_ACCT = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT
        self.SWIFT_BENEF_BANK = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK_BIC
        self.SWIFT_REMIT_INFO = self.WS_PURPOSE

    def transmit_wire(self):
        """
        24330-TRANSMIT-WIRE.
        CALL 'SWIFTSEND' USING WS-SWIFT-MESSAGE 
            WS-SWIFT-RESPONSE
        IF SWIFT-STATUS = 'ACK'
            MOVE 'SENT' TO WS-WIRE-STATUS
        ELSE
            MOVE 'FAILED' TO WS-WIRE-STATUS
            PERFORM 24350-REVERSE-DEBIT
        END-IF.
        """
        self.call_swiftsend()
        if self.SWIFT_STATUS == 'ACK':
            self.WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'SENT'
        else:
            self.WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'FAILED'
            self.reverse_debit()

    def call_swiftsend(self):
        """CALL 'SWIFTSEND' USING WS-SWIFT-MESSAGE WS-SWIFT-RESPONSE"""
        # Placeholder for SWIFTSEND call.  In a real system, this would involve sending the SWIFT message to a SWIFT network gateway.
        # Example:
        # self.WS_SWIFT_RESPONSE = swiftsend(self.WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE)
        # For now, we'll simulate an ACK 80% of the time.
        import random
        if random.random() < 0.8:
            self.SWIFT_STATUS = 'ACK'
        else:
            self.SWIFT_STATUS = 'NACK'

    def reverse_debit(self):
        """
        24350-REVERSE-DEBIT.
        ADD WS-WIRE-AMOUNT TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        ADD WS-WIRE-FEE TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT.
        """
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += self.WS_WIRE_FEE
        self.update_account()

    def record_wire(self):
        """
        24340-RECORD-WIRE.
        INITIALIZE WS-WIRE-RECORD
        MOVE WS-WIRE-REF TO WIRE-REF
        MOVE WS-WIRE-AMOUNT TO WIRE-AMOUNT
        MOVE WS-WIRE-STATUS TO WIRE-STATUS
        MOVE WS-ORIGINATOR-ACCOUNT TO WIRE-FROM-ACCT
        MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-ACCOUNT TO WIRE-TO-ACCT
        MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO WIRE-DATE
        WRITE WIRE-RECORD FROM WS-WIRE-RECORD.
        """
        wire_record = {} # replace with actual type
        wire_record["wire_ref"] = self.WS_WIRE_REF
        wire_record["wire_amount"] = self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
        wire_record["wire_status"] = self.WS_WIRE_STATUS
        wire_record["wire_from_acct"] = self.WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT
        wire_record["wire_to_acct"] = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT
        wire_record["wire_date"] = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WIRE_RECORD.append(wire_record) #placeholder

    def write_wire_record(self):
        """WRITE WIRE-RECORD FROM WS-WIRE-RECORD"""
        pass #placeholder

    def send_confirmation(self):
        """
        24400-SEND-CONFIRMATION.
        MOVE 'WIRE-CONFIRM' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
        MOVE 'EMAIL' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
        STRING 'Wire transfer ' DELIMITED SIZE
                WS-WIRE-REF DELIMITED SIZE
                ' completed' DELIMITED SIZE
            INTO WS-NOTIF-SUBJECT
        PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION.
        """
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'WIRE-CONFIRM'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'Wire transfer {self.WS_WIRE_REF} completed'
        self.WS_NOTIF_BODY = self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT #subject and body are the same here
        self.send_notification()

    def reject_wire(self):
        """
        24500-REJECT-WIRE.
        MOVE 'REJECTED' TO WS-WIRE-STATUS
        INITIALIZE WS-WIRE-REJECT-REC
        MOVE WS-WIRE-REF TO REJECT-WIRE-REF
        MOVE WS-WIRE-REJECT TO REJECT-REASON
        MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO REJECT-DATE
        WRITE WIRE-REJECT-RECORD FROM WS-WIRE-REJECT-REC
        MOVE 'WIRE-REJECTED' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
        PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION.
        """
        self.WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'REJECTED'
        reject_record = {} # replace with actual type
        reject_record["reject_wire_ref"] = self.WS_WIRE_REF
        reject_record["reject_reason"] = self.WS_WIRE_REJECT
        reject_record["reject_date"] = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WIRE_REJECT_RECORD.append(reject_record)
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'WIRE-REJECTED'
        self.WS_NOTIF_BODY = f"Wire transfer rejected: {self.WS_WIRE_REJECT}"
        self.send_notification()

    def write_wire_reject_record(self):
        """WRITE WIRE-REJECT-RECORD FROM WS-WIRE-REJECT-REC"""
        pass #placeholder

    def ach_processing(self):
        """
        25000-ACH-PROCESSING.
        PERFORM 25100-RECEIVE-ACH-FILE
        PERFORM 25200-VALIDATE-ACH-ENTRIES
        PERFORM 25300-PROCESS-ACH-CREDITS
        PERFORM 25400-PROCESS-ACH-DEBITS
        PERFORM 25500-GENERATE-ACH-RETURN.
        """
        self.receive_ach_file()
        self.validate_ach_entries()
        self.process_ach_credits()
        self.process_ach_debits()

    def validate_stop_request(self) -> None:
        """29100-VALIDATE-STOP-REQUEST."""
        self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'Y'
        if self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER == 0:
            self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED'
        if self.WS_CHECK_ALREADY_CLEARED == 'Y':
            self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED'

    def create_stop_order(self) -> None:
        """29200-CREATE-STOP-ORDER."""
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD = StopRecord()
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_account = self.ACCT_ID
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_check_number = str(self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER)
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_amount = self.WS_CHECK_AMOUNT
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_payee = self.WS_PAYEE_NAME
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_effective_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_expiry_date = self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 180
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_status = 'A'
        self.write_stop_record(self.WS_STOP_RECORD)

    def apply_stop_fee(self) -> None:
        """29300-APPLY-STOP-FEE."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_STOP_PAYMENT_FEE
        self.update_account()
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'STOP-PAYMENT'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'Stop payment placed on check #{self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER}'
        self.send_notification()

    def safe_deposit_box(self) -> None:
        """30000-SAFE-DEPOSIT-BOX."""
        self.box_rental()
        self.box_access()
        self.box_drilling()
        self.box_billing()

    def box_rental(self) -> None:
        """30100-BOX-RENTAL."""
        if self.WS_RENTAL_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.check_availability()
            if self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE == 'Y':
                self.assign_box()
                self.create_rental_agreement()

    def check_availability(self) -> None:
        """30110-CHECK-AVAILABILITY."""
        self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'N'
        for self.WS_BOX_IDX in range(1, self.WS_TOTAL_BOXES + 1):
            if (self.WS_BOX_IDX -1) < len(self.BOXES):
                if self.BOXES[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1].status == 'A':
                    if self.BOXES[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1].size == self.WS_REQUESTED_SIZE:
                        self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'Y'
                        self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX = self.WS_BOX_IDX
                        break

    def assign_box(self) -> None:
        """30120-ASSIGN-BOX."""
        self.BOXES[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1].status = 'R'
        self.BOXES[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1].renter = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        self.BOXES[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX - 1].rental_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE

    def create_rental_agreement(self) -> None:
        """30130-CREATE-RENTAL-AGREEMENT."""
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT = RentalAgreement()
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_box_number = str(self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX)
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_customer = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_start_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_annual_fee = self.WS_BOX_SIZE_FEE.get(self.WS_REQUESTED_SIZE, Decimal("0.00"))
        self.write_rental_record(self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT)

    def box_access(self) -> None:
        """30200-BOX-ACCESS."""
        if self.WS_ACCESS_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.verify_renter()
            if self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                self.log_access()
                self.escort_to_vault()

    def verify_renter(self) -> None:
        """30210-VERIFY-RENTER."""
        self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'N'
        if (self.WS_BOX_NUMBER - 1) < len(self.BOXES):
            if self.BOXES[self.WS_BOX_NUMBER - 1].renter == self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID:
                if self.WS_ID_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                    if self.WS_KEY_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                        self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'Y'

    def log_access(self) -> None:
        """30220-LOG-ACCESS."""
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG = AccessLog()
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_box_number = str(self.WS_BOX_NUMBER)
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_customer = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_time = datetime.now().strftime("%H%M%S")  # Or appropriate time format
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_type = 'ENTRY'
        self.write_access_log_record(self.WS_ACCESS_LOG)

    def escort_to_vault(self) -> None:
        """30230-ESCORT-TO-VAULT."""
        self.WS_DISPLAY_MSG = 'VAULT ACCESS GRANTED'
        self.display_msg(self.WS_DISPLAY_MSG)

    def box_drilling(self) -> None:
        """30300-BOX-DRILLING."""
        if self.WS_DRILLING_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.validate_drilling_auth()
            if self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED == 'Y':
                self.schedule_drilling()
                self.notify_renter()

    def validate_drilling_auth(self) -> None:
        """30310-VALIDATE-DRILLING-AUTH."""
        self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'N'
        if self.WS_RENT_DELINQUENT_MONTHS >= 12:
            self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'
        if self.WS_COURT_ORDER == 'Y':
            self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'
        if self.WS_DECEASED_RENTER == 'Y':
            if self.WS_EXECUTOR_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'

    def schedule_drilling(self) -> None:
        """30320-SCHEDULE-DRILLING."""
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD = DrillingRecord()
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD.drill_box_number = str(self.WS_BOX_NUMBER)
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD.drill_reason = self.WS_DRILLING_REASON
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD.drill_scheduled_date = self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 30
        self.write_drilling_record(self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD)

    def notify_renter(self) -> None:
        """30330-NOTIFY-RENTER."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'BOX-DRILLING'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'MAIL'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Important notice regarding your safe deposit box'
        self.send_notification()

    def box_billing(self) -> None:
        """30400-BOX-BILLING."""
        for self.WS_BOX_IDX in range(1, self.WS_TOTAL_BOXES + 1):
            if (self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1) < len(self.BOXES):
                if self.BOXES[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1].status == 'R':
                    if self.BOXES[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1].renewal_due == 'Y':
                        self.charge_annual_fee()

    def charge_annual_fee(self) -> None:
        """30410-CHARGE-ANNUAL-FEE."""
        self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID = self.BOXES[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1].renter
        self.WS_FEE_AMOUNT = self.BOXES[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1].annual_fee
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_FEE_AMOUNT
        self.update_account()
        self.BOXES[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1].next_renewal = self.BOXES[self.WS_BOX_IDX - 1].next_renewal + 10000

    def merchant_services(self) -> None:
        """31000-MERCHANT-SERVICES."""
        self.process_authorization()
        self.capture_transaction()
        self.process_settlement()
        self.handle_chargeback()

    def process_authorization(self) -> None:
        """31100-PROCESS-AUTHORIZATION."""
        self.validate_card()
        if self.WS_CARD_VALID == 'Y':
            self.check_fraud_score()
            if self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED == 'Y':
                self.check_available_credit()
                if self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE == 'Y':
                    self.approve_auth()
                else:
                    self.decline_auth()
            else:
                self.decline_auth()
        else:
            self.decline_auth()

    def validate_card(self) -> None:
        """31110-VALIDATE-CARD."""
        self.WS_CARD_VALID = 'N'
        self.check_luhn()
        if self.WS_LUHN_VALID == 'Y':
            self.check_expiry()
            if self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED == 'Y':
                self.check_cvv()
                if self.WS_CVV_VALID == 'Y':
                    self.WS_CARD_VALID = 'Y'

    def check_luhn(self) -> None:
        """31115-CHECK-LUHN."""
        self.WS_LUHN_SUM = 0
        card_number = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
        for i in range(16, 0, -1):
            self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT = int(card_number[i-1])
            if (17 - i) % 2 == 0:
                self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT *= 2
                if self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT > 9:
                    self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT -= 9
            self.WS_LUHN_SUM += self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT

        if self.WS_LUHN_SUM % 10 == 0:
            self.WS_LUHN_VALID = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_LUHN_VALID = 'N'

    def check_expiry(self) -> None:
        """31116-CHECK-EXPIRY."""
        if self.WS_AUTH_EXPIRY_DATE >= self.WS_PROCESS_DATE:
            self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'N'

    def check_cvv(self) -> None:
        """31117-CHECK-CVV."""
        # Assuming CVVVERIFY is an external function
        self.WS_CVV_RESULT = cvvverify(self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, self.WS_AUTH_CVV)
        if self.WS_CVV_RESULT == 'M':
            self.WS_CVV_VALID = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_CVV_VALID = 'N'

    def check_fraud_score(self) -> None:
        """31120-CHECK-FRAUD-SCORE."""
        # Assuming FRAUDCHECK is an external function and returns a FraudResponse object
        self.FRAUD_RESPONSE = fraudcheck(self.WS_AUTH_REQUEST)
        self.FRAUD_SCORE = self.FRAUD_RESPONSE.fraud_score

        if self.FRAUD_SCORE < 70:
            self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'N'
            self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = self.FRAUD_RESPONSE.decline_code

    def check_available_credit(self) -> None:
        """31130-CHECK-AVAILABLE-CREDIT."""
        self.WS_SEARCH_KEY = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
        self.WS_CARD_ACCOUNT_REC = self.read_card_account_file(self.WS_SEARCH_KEY) # Assuming file read function
        if self.WS_CARD_ACCOUNT_REC.available_credit >= self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT:
            self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'Y'
            self.WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT = self.WS_CARD_ACCOUNT_REC.available_credit
        else:
            self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'N'
            self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = '51'

    def approve_auth(self) -> None:
        """31140-APPROVE-AUTH."""
        self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE = '00'
        self.generate_auth_code()
        self.WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT -= self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
        self.record_authorization()

    def generate_auth_code(self) -> None:
        """31145-GENERATE-AUTH-CODE."""
        self.WS_AUTH_CODE = int(random.random() * 999999)
        self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE = str(self.WS_AUTH_CODE)

    def record_authorization(self) -> None:
        """31146-RECORD-AUTHORIZATION."""
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD = AuthRecord()
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_card = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_amount = self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_code = self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_time = datetime.now().strftime("%H%M%S")  # Or appropriate time format
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_merchant = self.WS_MERCHANT_ID
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_status = 'P'
        self.write_auth_record(self.WS_AUTH_RECORD)

    def decline_auth(self) -> None:
        """31150-DECLINE-AUTH."""
        self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE = self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD = DeclineRecord()
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_rec_card = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_rec_amount = self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_rec_code = self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_rec_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.write_decline_record(self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD)

    def capture_transaction(self) -> None:
        """31200-CAPTURE-TRANSACTION."""
        if self.WS_CAPTURE_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.validate_auth_code()
            if self.WS_AUTH_VALID == 'Y':
                self.create_capture_record()

    def validate_auth_code(self) -> None:
        """31210-VALIDATE-AUTH-CODE."""
        self.WS_AUTH_VALID = 'N'
        self.AUTH_CODE = self.WS_CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE
        self.WS_AUTH_REC = self.read_auth_file(self.AUTH_CODE)  # Read by AUTH_CODE
        if self.WS_AUTH_REC: # Assuming empty object/None indicates INVALID KEY
            if self.WS_AUTH_REC.auth_rec_status == 'P':
                self.WS_AUTH_VALID = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_AUTH_VALID = 'N'

    def create_capture_record(self) -> None:
        """31220-CREATE-CAPTURE-RECORD."""
        self.WS_AUTH_REC.auth_rec_status = 'C'
        self.rewrite_auth_record(self.WS_AUTH_REC)
        self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD = CaptureRecord()
        self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD.capture_card = self.WS_AUTH_REC.auth_rec_card
        self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD.capture_amount = self.WS_CAPTURE_AMOUNT
        self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD.capture_auth_code = self.WS_CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE
        self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD.capture_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.write_capture_record(self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD)

    def process_settlement(self) -> None:
        """31300-PROCESS-SETTLEMENT."""
        self.batch_transactions()
        self.calculate_fees()
        self.create_funding_record()
        self.send_settlement_file()

    def batch_transactions(self) -> None:
        """31310-BATCH-TRANSACTIONS."""
        self.WS_BATCH_TOTAL = Decimal("0.00")

def update_cfp_document(ws_cfp_status: str, ws_available_funding: Decimal, ws_stressed_outflows: Decimal, cfp_record: CfpRecord) -> None:
    """
    33330-UPDATE-CFP-DOCUMENT.
    MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-CFP-UPDATE-DATE
    MOVE WS-CFP-STATUS TO CFP-OVERALL-STATUS
    MOVE WS-AVAILABLE-FUNDING TO CFP-TOTAL-SOURCES
    MOVE WS-STRESSED-OUTFLOWS TO CFP-STRESS-NEEDS
    REWRITE CFP-RECORD FROM WS-CFP-DOCUMENT.

        cfp_update_date=date.today(),

        cfp_stress_needs=ws_stressed_outflows
    )
    # Assume a function to rewrite the record based on the updated WS structure
    rewrite_cfp_record(cfp_record, ws_cfp_document)

def capital_management(
    ws_common_stock: Decimal,
    ws_retained_earnings: Decimal,
    ws_aoci: Decimal,
    ws_goodwill: Decimal,
    ws_intangibles: Decimal,
    ws_dta_deduction: Decimal,
    ws_sub_debt: Decimal,
    ws_alll_eligible: Decimal,
    ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal,
    ws_total_assets: Decimal,
    ws_cash_position: Decimal,
    ws_govt_securities: Decimal,
    ws_bank_deposits: Decimal,
    ws_residential_mortgages: Decimal,
    ws_commercial_loans: Decimal,
    ws_consumer_loans: Decimal,
    ws_trading_assets: Decimal,
    ws_market_risk_factor: Decimal,
    ws_gross_income: Decimal,
    ws_operational_factor: Decimal,
    ws_growth_rate: Decimal,
    ws_target_ratio: Decimal,
    ws_retained_earnings_proj: Decimal,
    ws_sub_debt_capacity: Decimal,
    ws_loan_portfolio: Decimal,
    ws_stress_lgd: Decimal,
    ws_stress_pd: Decimal,
    ws_min_capital_ratio: Decimal,
    ws_notification_type: str,
    ws_notification_channel: str,
    ws_notification_subject: str,
    send_notification: callable,
    capital_plan_record: "CapitalPlanRecord",
    gl_asset: bool,
    gl_liability: bool,
    gl_equity: bool,
    handle_error: callable,
    ws_end_of_month: str,
    gl_revenue: bool,
    gl_expense: bool,
    ws_process_date: date,
    trial_balance_file: str,
    y9c_record: str,
    subsidiary_file: str,
    intercompany_file: str,
    ccar_loan_data: Decimal,
    ccar_sec_data: Decimal,
    ccar_trading_data: Decimal,
    transaction_file: str,
    sar_pending_file: str,
    customer_file: str,
    bank_statement_file: str,
    book_transactions: str,
    subledger_file: str,
    gl_master_file:str,
    je_gl_account: list,
    je_debit: list,
    je_credit: list,
    gl_search_key:str

) -> None:
    """
    34000-CAPITAL-MANAGEMENT.
    PERFORM 34100-CALCULATE-CAPITAL-RATIOS
    PERFORM 34200-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
    PERFORM 34300-CAPITAL-PLANNING
    PERFORM 34400-STRESS-TESTING.
    """
    calculate_capital_ratios(ws_common_stock, ws_retained_earnings, ws_aoci, ws_goodwill, ws_intangibles, ws_dta_deduction, ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_total_assets)
    risk_weighted_assets(ws_cash_position, ws_govt_securities, ws_bank_deposits, ws_residential_mortgages, ws_commercial_loans, ws_consumer_loans, ws_trading_assets, ws_market_risk_factor, ws_gross_income, ws_operational_factor)
    capital_planning(ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_growth_rate, ws_target_ratio, ws_retained_earnings_proj, ws_sub_debt_capacity,capital_plan_record)
    stress_testing(ws_loan_portfolio, ws_stress_lgd, ws_stress_pd, ws_min_capital_ratio, ws_notification_type, ws_notification_channel, ws_notification_subject, send_notification)
    general_ledger(gl_asset, gl_liability, gl_equity, handle_error,ws_end_of_month, gl_revenue, gl_expense, ws_process_date, trial_balance_file, je_gl_account, je_debit, je_credit, gl_master_file)
    regulatory_reporting(y9c_record, subsidiary_file, intercompany_file,ccar_loan_data,ccar_sec_data,ccar_trading_data,transaction_file, sar_pending_file, customer_file)
    reconciliation(bank_statement_file, book_transactions, subledger_file, gl_search_key, gl_master_file)

def calculate_capital_ratios(
    ws_common_stock: Decimal,
    ws_retained_earnings: Decimal,
    ws_aoci: Decimal,
    ws_goodwill: Decimal,
    ws_intangibles: Decimal,
    ws_dta_deduction: Decimal,
    ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal,
    ws_total_assets: Decimal
) -> None:
    """
    34100-CALCULATE-CAPITAL-RATIOS.
    PERFORM 34110-CALCULATE-TIER1
    PERFORM 34120-CALCULATE-TIER2
    PERFORM 34130-CALCULATE-RATIOS.
    """
    calculate_tier1(ws_common_stock, ws_retained_earnings, ws_aoci, ws_goodwill, ws_intangibles, ws_dta_deduction)
    calculate_tier2(ws_sub_debt=Decimal("0"), ws_alll_eligible=Decimal("0"))
    calculate_ratios(ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_total_assets)

WS_TIER1_CAPITAL: Decimal = Decimal("0") # Global scope for sharing among methods

def calculate_tier1(
    ws_common_stock: Decimal,
    ws_retained_earnings: Decimal,
    ws_aoci: Decimal,
    ws_goodwill: Decimal,
    ws_intangibles: Decimal,
    ws_dta_deduction: Decimal
) -> None:
    """
    34110-CALCULATE-TIER1.
    MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
    ADD WS-COMMON-STOCK TO WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
    ADD WS-RETAINED-EARNINGS TO WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
    ADD WS-AOCI TO WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
    SUBTRACT WS-GOODWILL FROM WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
    SUBTRACT WS-INTANGIBLES FROM WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
    SUBTRACT WS-DTA-DEDUCTION FROM WS-TIER1-CAPITAL.
    """
    global WS_TIER1_CAPITAL
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL = Decimal("0")
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += ws_common_stock
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += ws_retained_earnings
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += ws_aoci
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= ws_goodwill
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= ws_intangibles
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= ws_dta_deduction

WS_TIER2_CAPITAL: Decimal = Decimal("0")
WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL: Decimal = Decimal("0")

def calculate_tier2(ws_sub_debt: Decimal, ws_alll_eligible: Decimal) -> None:
    """
    34120-CALCULATE-TIER2.
    MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TIER2-CAPITAL
    ADD WS-SUB-DEBT TO WS-TIER2-CAPITAL
    ADD WS-ALLL-ELIGIBLE TO WS-TIER2-CAPITAL
    COMPUTE WS-TOTAL-CAPITAL = 
       WS-TIER1-CAPITAL + WS-TIER2-CAPITAL.
    """
    global WS_TIER2_CAPITAL, WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL
    WS_TIER2_CAPITAL = Decimal("0")
    WS_TIER2_CAPITAL += ws_sub_debt
    WS_TIER2_CAPITAL += ws_alll_eligible
    WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL = WS_TIER1_CAPITAL + WS_TIER2_CAPITAL

WS_CET1_RATIO: Decimal = Decimal("0")
WS_CAPITAL_RATIO: Decimal = Decimal("0")
WS_LEVERAGE_RATIO: Decimal = Decimal("0")

def calculate_ratios(ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal, ws_total_assets: Decimal) -> None:
    """
    34130-CALCULATE-RATIOS.
    IF WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS > 0
       COMPUTE WS-CET1-RATIO = 
          (WS-TIER1-CAPITAL / WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS) * 100
       COMPUTE WS-CAPITAL-RATIO = 
          (WS-TOTAL-CAPITAL / WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS) * 100
    END-IF
    IF WS-TOTAL-ASSETS > 0
       COMPUTE WS-LEVERAGE-RATIO = 
          (WS-TIER1-CAPITAL / WS-TOTAL-ASSETS) * 100
    END-IF.
    """
    global WS_CET1_RATIO, WS_CAPITAL_RATIO, WS_LEVERAGE_RATIO
    if ws_risk_weighted_assets > Decimal("0"):
        WS_CET1_RATIO = (WS_TIER1_CAPITAL / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * Decimal("100")
        WS_CAPITAL_RATIO = (WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * Decimal("100")
    if ws_total_assets > Decimal("0"):
        WS_LEVERAGE_RATIO = (WS_TIER1_CAPITAL / ws_total_assets) * Decimal("100")

WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS: Decimal = Decimal("0")

def risk_weighted_assets(
    ws_cash_position: Decimal,
    ws_govt_securities: Decimal,
    ws_bank_deposits: Decimal,
    ws_residential_mortgages: Decimal,
    ws_commercial_loans: Decimal,
    ws_consumer_loans: Decimal,
    ws_trading_assets: Decimal,
    ws_market_risk_factor: Decimal,
    ws_gross_income: Decimal,
    ws_operational_factor: Decimal
) -> None:
    """
    34200-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS.
    MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
    PERFORM 34210-CREDIT-RWA
    PERFORM 34220-MARKET-RWA
    PERFORM 34230-OPERATIONAL-RWA.
    """
    global WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS = Decimal("0")
    credit_rwa(ws_cash_position, ws_govt_securities, ws_bank_deposits, ws_residential_mortgages, ws_commercial_loans, ws_consumer_loans)
    market_rwa(ws_trading_assets, ws_market_risk_factor)
    operational_rwa(ws_gross_income, ws_operational_factor)

def credit_rwa(
    ws_cash_position: Decimal,
    ws_govt_securities: Decimal,
    ws_bank_deposits: Decimal,
    ws_residential_mortgages: Decimal,
    ws_commercial_loans: Decimal,
    ws_consumer_loans: Decimal
) -> None:
    """
    34210-CREDIT-RWA.
    COMPUTE WS-CASH-RWA = WS-CASH-POSITION * 0.00
    COMPUTE WS-GOVT-RWA = WS-GOVT-SECURITIES * 0.00
    COMPUTE WS-BANK-RWA = WS-BANK-DEPOSITS * 0.20
    COMPUTE WS-MORTGAGE-RWA = WS-RESIDENTIAL-MORTGAGES * 0.50
    COMPUTE WS-COMMERCIAL-RWA = WS-COMMERCIAL-LOANS * 1.00
    COMPUTE WS-CONSUMER-RWA = WS-CONSUMER-LOANS * 1.00
    ADD WS-CASH-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
    ADD WS-GOVT-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
    ADD WS-BANK-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
    ADD WS-MORTGAGE-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
    ADD WS-COMMERCIAL-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
    ADD WS-CONSUMER-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS.
    """
    global WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS
    ws_cash_rwa = ws_cash_position * Decimal("0.00")
    ws_govt_rwa = ws_govt_securities * Decimal("0.00")
    ws_bank_rwa = ws_bank_deposits * Decimal("0.20")
    ws_mortgage_rwa = ws_residential_mortgages * Decimal("0.50")
    ws_commercial_rwa = ws_commercial_loans * Decimal("1.00")
    ws_consumer_rwa = ws_consumer_loans * Decimal("1.00")
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += ws_cash_rwa
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += ws_govt_rwa
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += ws_bank_rwa
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += ws_mortgage_rwa
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += ws_commercial_rwa
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += ws_consumer_rwa

def market_rwa(ws_trading_assets: Decimal, ws_market_risk_factor: Decimal) -> None:
    """
    34220-MARKET-RWA.
    COMPUTE WS-MARKET-RWA = 
       WS-TRADING-ASSETS * WS-MARKET-RISK-FACTOR
    ADD WS-MARKET-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS.
    """
    global WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS
    ws_market_rwa = ws_trading_assets * ws_market_risk_factor
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += ws_market_rwa

def operational_rwa(ws_gross_income: Decimal, ws_operational_factor: Decimal) -> None:
    """
    34230-OPERATIONAL-RWA.
    COMPUTE WS-OPERATIONAL-RWA = 
       WS-GROSS-INCOME * WS-OPERATIONAL-FACTOR * 12.5
    ADD WS-OPERATIONAL-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS.
    """
    global WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS
    ws_operational_rwa = ws_gross_income * ws_operational_factor * Decimal("12.5")
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += ws_operational_rwa

@dataclass
class CapitalPlanRecord:
    plan_recommended_action: str = ""
    plan_gap_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")

WS_PROJECTED_RWA: Decimal = Decimal("0")
WS_REQUIRED_CAPITAL: Decimal = Decimal("0")
WS_CAPITAL_GAP: Decimal = Decimal("0")

def capital_planning(ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal, ws_growth_rate: Decimal, ws_target_ratio: Decimal, ws_retained_earnings_proj: Decimal, ws_sub_debt_capacity: Decimal, capital_plan_record: "CapitalPlanRecord") -> None:
    """
    34300-CAPITAL-PLANNING.
    PERFORM 34310-PROJECT-CAPITAL-NEEDS
    PERFORM 34320-IDENTIFY-CAPITAL-ACTIONS
    PERFORM 34330-UPDATE-CAPITAL-PLAN.
    """
    project_capital_needs(ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_growth_rate, ws_target_ratio)
    identify_capital_actions(ws_retained_earnings_proj, ws_sub_debt_capacity)
    update_capital_plan(capital_plan_record)

def project_capital_needs(ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal, ws_growth_rate: Decimal, ws_target_ratio: Decimal) -> None:
    """
    34310-PROJECT-CAPITAL-NEEDS.
    COMPUTE WS-PROJECTED-RWA = 
       WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS * (1 + WS-GROWTH-RATE)
    COMPUTE WS-REQUIRED-CAPITAL = 
       WS-PROJECTED-RWA * WS-TARGET-RATIO / 100
    COMPUTE WS-CAPITAL-GAP = 
       WS-REQUIRED-CAPITAL - WS-TOTAL-CAPITAL.
    """
    global WS_PROJECTED_RWA, WS_REQUIRED_CAPITAL, WS_CAPITAL_GAP
    WS_PROJECTED_RWA = ws_risk_weighted_assets * (Decimal("1") + ws_growth_rate)
    WS_REQUIRED_CAPITAL = WS_PROJECTED_RWA * ws_target_ratio / Decimal("100")
    WS_CAPITAL_GAP = WS_REQUIRED_CAPITAL - WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL

WS_CAPITAL_ACTION: str = ""

def identify_capital_actions(ws_retained_earnings_proj: Decimal, ws_sub_debt_capacity: Decimal) -> None:
    """
    34320-IDENTIFY-CAPITAL-ACTIONS.
    IF WS-CAPITAL-GAP > 0
       EVALUATE TRUE
          WHEN WS-CAPITAL-GAP <= WS-RETAINED-EARNINGS-PROJ
             MOVE 'ORGANIC GROWTH' TO WS-CAPITAL-ACTION
          WHEN WS-CAPITAL-GAP <= WS-SUB-DEBT-CAPACITY
             MOVE 'SUB DEBT ISSUANCE' TO WS-CAPITAL-ACTION
          WHEN OTHER
             MOVE 'EQUITY RAISE' TO WS-CAPITAL-ACTION
       END-EVALUATE
    ELSE
       MOVE 'NO ACTION NEEDED' TO WS-CAPITAL-ACTION
    END-IF.
    """
    global WS_CAPITAL_ACTION
    if WS_CAPITAL_GAP > Decimal("0"):
        if WS_CAPITAL_GAP <= ws_retained_earnings_proj:
            WS_CAPITAL_ACTION = "ORGANIC GROWTH"
        elif WS_CAPITAL_GAP <= ws_sub_debt_capacity:
            WS_CAPITAL_ACTION = "SUB DEBT ISSUANCE"
        else:
            WS_CAPITAL_ACTION = "EQUITY RAISE"
    else:
        WS_CAPITAL_ACTION = "NO ACTION NEEDED"

def update_capital_plan(capital_plan_record: "CapitalPlanRecord") -> None:
    """
    34330-UPDATE-CAPITAL-PLAN.
    MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-PLAN-UPDATE-DATE
    MOVE WS-CAPITAL-ACTION TO PLAN-RECOMMENDED-ACTION
    MOVE WS-CAPITAL-GAP TO PLAN-GAP-AMOUNT
    REWRITE CAPITAL-PLAN-RECORD FROM WS-CAPITAL-PLAN.
    """
    capital_plan_record.plan_recommended_action = WS_CAPITAL_ACTION
    capital_plan_record.plan_gap_amount = WS_CAPITAL_GAP
    #Assume a function to rewrite the record based on the updated WS structure
    rewrite_capital_plan_record(capital_plan_record)

def stress_testing(ws_loan_portfolio: Decimal, ws_stress_lgd: Decimal, ws_stress_pd: Decimal, ws_min_capital_ratio: Decimal, ws_notification_type: str, ws_notification_channel: str, ws_notification_subject: str, send_notification: callable) -> None:
    """
    34400-STRESS-TESTING.
    PERFORM 34410-RUN-BASELINE
    PERFORM 34420-RUN-ADVERSE
    PERFORM 34430-RUN-SEVERELY-ADVERSE
    PERFORM 34440-COMPILE-RESULTS.
    """
    run_baseline(ws_loan_portfolio, ws_stress_lgd, ws_stress_pd, ws_min_capital_ratio)
    run_adverse(ws_loan_portfolio, ws_stress_lgd, ws_stress_pd, ws_min_capital_ratio)
    run_severely_adverse(ws_loan_portfolio, ws_stress_lgd, ws_stress_pd, ws_min_capital_ratio)
    compile_results(ws_notification_type, ws_notification_channel, ws_notification_subject, send_notification)

WS_SCENARIO_NAME: str = ""
WS_RATE_SHOCK: Decimal = Decimal("0")
WS_GDP_CHANGE: Decimal = Decimal("0")
WS_UNEMPLOYMENT_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0")
WS_HOUSING_DECLINE: Decimal = Decimal("0")

def run_baseline(ws_loan_portfolio: Decimal, ws_stress_lgd: Decimal, ws_stress_pd: Decimal, ws_min_capital_ratio: Decimal) -> None:
    """
    34410-RUN-BASELINE.
    MOVE 'BASELINE' TO WS-SCENARIO-NAME
    MOVE 0.00 TO WS-RATE-SHOCK
    MOVE 2.50 TO WS-GDP-CHANGE
    MOVE 4.00 TO WS-UNEMPLOYMENT-RATE
    MOVE 0.00 TO WS-HOUSING-DECLINE
    PERFORM 34450-CALCULATE-STRESS-IMPACT.
    """
    global WS_SCENARIO_NAME, WS_RATE_SHOCK, WS_GDP_CHANGE, WS_UNEMPLOYMENT_RATE, WS_HOUSING_DECLINE
    WS_SCENARIO_NAME = "BASELINE"
    WS_RATE_SHOCK = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_GDP_CHANGE = Decimal("2.50")
    WS_UNEMPLOYMENT_RATE = Decimal("4.00")
    WS_HOUSING_DECLINE = Decimal("0.00")
    calculate_stress_impact(ws_loan_portfolio, ws_stress_lgd, ws_stress_pd, ws_min_capital_ratio)

def run_adverse(ws_loan_portfolio: Decimal, ws_stress_lgd: Decimal, ws_stress_pd: Decimal, ws_min_capital_ratio: Decimal) -> None:
    """
    34420-RUN-ADVERSE.
    MOVE 'ADVERSE' TO WS-SCENARIO-NAME
    MOVE 2.00 TO WS-RATE-SHOCK
    MOVE -1.50 TO WS-GDP-CHANGE
    MOVE 7.00 TO WS-UNEMPLOYMENT-RATE
    MOVE -15.00 TO WS-HOUSING-DECLINE
    PERFORM 34450-CALCULATE-STRESS-IMPACT.
    """

    WS_SCENARIO_NAME = "ADVERSE"
    WS_RATE_SHOCK = Decimal("2.00")
    WS_GDP_CHANGE = Decimal("-1.50")
    WS_UNEMPLOYMENT_RATE = Decimal("7.00")
    WS_HOUSING_DECLINE = Decimal("-15.00")
    calculate_stress_impact(ws_loan_portfolio, ws_stress_lgd, ws_stress_pd, ws_min_capital_ratio)

def run_severely_adverse(ws_loan_portfolio: Decimal, ws_stress_lgd: Decimal, ws_stress_pd: Decimal, ws_min_capital_ratio: Decimal) -> None:
    """
    34430-RUN-SEVERELY-ADVERSE.
    MOVE 'SEVERELY-ADVERSE' TO WS-SCENARIO-NAME
    MOVE 3.00 TO WS-RATE-SHOCK
    MOVE -6.00 TO WS-GDP-CHANGE

    WS_SCENARIO_NAME = "SEVERELY-ADVERSE"

    WS_GDP_CHANGE = Decimal("-6.00")
    WS_UNEMPLOYMENT_RATE = Decimal("10.00")
    WS_HOUSING_DECLINE = Decimal("-30.00")
    calculate_stress_impact(ws_loan_portfolio, ws_stress_lgd, ws_stress_pd, ws_min_capital_ratio)

WS_STRESS_PASS_FAIL: str = ""

def compile_results(ws_notification_type: str, ws_notification_channel: str, ws_notification_subject: str, send_notification: callable) -> None:
    """
    34440-COMPILE-RESULTS.
    DISPLAY 'STRESS TEST RESULTS COMPILED'
    IF WS-STRESS-PASS-FAIL = 'FAIL'
       PERFORM 34460-REMEDIATION-ACTIONS
    END-IF.
    """
    print("STRESS TEST RESULTS COMPILED")
    global WS_STRESS_PASS_FAIL
    if WS_STRESS_PASS_FAIL == "FAIL":
        remediation_actions(ws_notification_type, ws_notification_channel, ws_notification_subject, send_notification)

WS_CREDIT_LOSSES: Decimal = Decimal("0")
WS_MARKET_LOSSES: Decimal = Decimal("0")
WS_STRESS_LOSSES: Decimal = Decimal("0")
WS_STRESSED_CAPITAL: Decimal = Decimal("0")
WS_STRESSED_RATIO: Decimal = Decimal("0")

def calculate_stress_impact(ws_loan_portfolio: Decimal, ws_stress_lgd: Decimal, ws_stress_pd: Decimal, ws_min_capital_ratio: Decimal) -> None:
    """
    34450-CALCULATE-STRESS-IMPACT.
    COMPUTE WS-CREDIT-LOSSES = 
       WS-LOAN-PORTFOLIO * WS-STRESS-LGD * 
       WS-STRESS-PD
    COMPUTE WS-MARKET-LOSSES = 
       WS-TRADING-ASSETS * WS-RATE-SHOCK / 100
    COMPUTE WS-STRESS-LOSSES = 
       WS-CREDIT-LOSSES + WS-MARKET-LOSSES
    COMPUTE WS-STRESSED-CAPITAL = 
       WS-TOTAL-CAPITAL - WS-STRESS-LOSSES
    COMPUTE WS-STRESSED-RATIO = 
       (WS-STRESSED-CAPITAL / WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS) * 100
    IF WS-STRESSED-RATIO >= WS-MIN-CAPITAL-RATIO
       MOVE 'PASS' TO WS-STRESS-PASS-FAIL
    ELSE
       MOVE 'FAIL' TO WS-STRESS-PASS-FAIL
    END-IF.
    """
    global WS_CREDIT_LOSSES, WS_MARKET_LOSSES, WS_STRESS_LOSSES, WS_STRESSED_CAPITAL, WS_STRESSED_RATIO, WS_STRESS_PASS_FAIL
    WS_CREDIT_LOSSES = ws_loan_portfolio * ws_stress_lgd * ws_stress_pd
    WS_MARKET_LOSSES = Decimal("0") #assuming WS-TRADING-ASSETS is 0
    WS_STRESS_LOSSES = WS_CREDIT_LOSSES + WS_MARKET_LOSSES
    WS_STRESSED_CAPITAL = WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL - WS_STRESS_LOSSES
    WS_STRESSED_RATIO = (WS_STRESSED_CAPITAL / WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS) * Decimal("100")
    if WS_STRESSED_RATIO >= ws_min_capital_ratio:
        WS_STRESS_PASS_FAIL = "PASS"
    else:
        WS_STRESS_PASS_FAIL = "FAIL"

def remediation_actions(ws_notification_type: str, ws_notification_channel: str, ws_notification_subject: str, send_notification: callable) -> None:
    """
    34460-REMEDIATION-ACTIONS.
    MOVE 'STRESS-FAILURE' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
    MOVE 'EMAIL' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
    MOVE 'URGENT: Stress test failure - action required'
       TO WS-NOTIF-SUBJECT
    PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION.
    """
    notification_type = "STRESS-FAILURE"
    notification_channel = "EMAIL"
    notification_subject = "URGENT: Stress test failure - action required"
    send_notification(notification_type, notification_channel, notification_subject)

def general_ledger(gl_asset: bool, gl_liability: bool, gl_equity: bool, handle_error: callable,ws_end_of_month: str, gl_revenue: bool, gl_expense: bool, ws_process_date: date, trial_balance_file: str, je_gl_account: list, je_debit: list, je_credit: list, gl_master_file:str) -> None:
    """
    35000-GENERAL-LEDGER.
    PERFORM 35100-POST-JOURNAL-ENTRY
    PERFORM 35200-BALANCE-GL
    PERFORM 35300-CLOSE-PERIOD
    PERFORM 35400-GENERATE-TRIAL-BALANCE.
    """
    post_journal_entry(je_gl_account, je_debit, je_credit, gl_master_file)
    balance_gl(gl_asset, gl_liability, gl_equity, handle_error, gl_master_file)
    close_period(ws_end_of_month, gl_revenue, gl_expense, gl_master_file, ws_process_date)
    generate_trial_balance(trial_balance_file, gl_master_file, ws_process_date)

def post_journal_entry(je_gl_account: list, je_debit: list, je_credit: list, gl_master_file:str) -> None:
    """
    35100-POST-JOURNAL-ENTRY.
    PERFORM 35110-VALIDATE-JOURNAL-ENTRY
    IF WS-JE-VALID = 'Y'
       PERFORM 35120-POST-TO-ACCOUNTS
       PERFORM 35130-RECORD-POSTING
    END-IF.
    """
    ws_je_valid, ws_je_error = validate_journal_entry(je_debit, je_credit)
    if ws_je_valid == "Y":
        post_to_accounts(je_gl_account, je_debit, je_credit, gl_master_file)
        record_posting()

    CUST_BRANCH_VISITS: int = 0
    CUST_CALL_COUNT: int = 0
    CUST_ONLINE_TRANS: int = 0
    CUST_PROFITABILITY: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
