def main():
    """Main function to execute the translated COBOL code."""

    global WS_RECON_DIFF, WS_GL_CONTROL_BAL, WS_SUBLEDGER_TOTAL

    #           WS-GL-CONTROL-BAL - WS-SUBLEDGER-TOTAL
    if WS_RECON_DIFF != Decimal('0.00'):
        log_recon_exception()

def log_recon_exception():
    """37235-LOG-RECON-EXCEPTION."""
    global WS_RECON_EXCEPTION, WS_GL_ACCOUNT, WS_RECON_DIFF

    ws_recon_exception: WS_RECON_EXCEPTION = WS_RECON_EXCEPTION()
    #        MOVE WS-GL-ACCOUNT TO RECON-EXC-ACCOUNT
    ws_recon_exception.RECON_EXC_ACCOUNT = WS_GL_ACCOUNT
    #        MOVE WS-RECON-DIFF TO RECON-EXC-DIFF
    ws_recon_exception.RECON_EXC_DIFF = WS_RECON_DIFF
    #        MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO RECON-EXC-DATE
    ws_recon_exception.RECON_EXC_DATE = datetime.now()
    #        WRITE RECON-EXCEPTION-RECORD FROM WS-RECON-EXCEPTION
    write_record(RECON_EXCEPTION_RECORD, ws_recon_exception)

def intercompany_recon():
    """37300-INTERCOMPANY-RECON."""
    load_ic_balances()
    match_ic_pairs()
    report_ic_differences()

def load_ic_balances():
    """37310-LOAD-IC-BALANCES."""
    global WS_IC_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_IC_ARRAY, WS_IC_BALANCE

    #        MOVE ZEROES TO WS-IC-COUNT
    WS_IC_COUNT = 0
    #        PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        #     READ INTERCOMPANY-FILE INTO WS-IC-BALANCE
        ws_ic_balance: WS_IC_BALANCE = read_intercompany_file() # get from file
        if ws_ic_balance is None:
            #        AT END
            #           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            #        NOT AT END
            #           ADD 1 TO WS-IC-COUNT
            WS_IC_COUNT += 1
            #           MOVE WS-IC-BALANCE TO
            #              WS-IC-ARRAY(WS-IC-COUNT)
            WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_COUNT - 1] = ws_ic_balance
        #     END-READ
    #     END-PERFORM
    #     MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def read_intercompany_file() -> WS_IC_BALANCE | None:
    """Dummy function to simulate reading from INTERCOMPANY-FILE."""
    global WS_IC_BALANCE
    if WS_IC_COUNT < 5:
        return WS_IC_BALANCE(IC_FROM_ENTITY=f"FROM{WS_IC_COUNT}", IC_TO_ENTITY=f"TO{WS_IC_COUNT}", IC_AMOUNT=Decimal(str(WS_IC_COUNT * 100)))
    else:
        return None

def match_ic_pairs():
    """37320-MATCH-IC-PAIRS."""
    global WS_IC_IDX

    #     PERFORM VARYING WS-IC-IDX FROM 1 BY 1
    #        UNTIL WS-IC-IDX > WS-IC-COUNT
    WS_IC_IDX = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX <= WS_IC_COUNT:
        #        PERFORM 37325-FIND-IC-COUNTERPART
        find_ic_counterpart()
        WS_IC_IDX += 1
    #     END-PERFORM

def find_ic_counterpart():
    """37325-FIND-IC-COUNTERPART."""
    global WS_IC_IDX, WS_IC_IDX2, WS_SEARCH_FROM, WS_SEARCH_TO, WS_IC_DIFF, WS_IC_ARRAY

    #     MOVE IC-FROM-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX) TO WS-SEARCH-FROM
    WS_SEARCH_FROM = WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX - 1].IC_FROM_ENTITY
    #     MOVE IC-TO-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX) TO WS-SEARCH-TO
    WS_SEARCH_TO = WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX - 1].IC_TO_ENTITY
    #     PERFORM VARYING WS-IC-IDX2 FROM 1 BY 1
    #        UNTIL WS-IC-IDX2 > WS-IC-COUNT
    WS_IC_IDX2 = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX2 <= WS_IC_COUNT:
        #     IF IC-FROM-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX2) = WS-SEARCH-TO
        if WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].IC_FROM_ENTITY == WS_SEARCH_TO:
            #        IF IC-TO-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX2) = WS-SEARCH-FROM
            if WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].IC_TO_ENTITY == WS_SEARCH_FROM:
                #           COMPUTE WS-IC-DIFF =
                #              IC-AMOUNT(WS-IC-IDX) +
                #              IC-AMOUNT(WS-IC-IDX2)
                WS_IC_DIFF = WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX - 1].IC_AMOUNT + WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].IC_AMOUNT
                #           IF WS-IC-DIFF NOT = ZEROES
                if WS_IC_DIFF != Decimal('0.00'):
                    #              PERFORM 37326-LOG-IC-DIFF
                    log_ic_diff()
                #           END-IF
                #           EXIT PERFORM
                break
            #        END-IF
        #     END-IF
        WS_IC_IDX2 += 1
    #     END-PERFORM

def log_ic_diff():
    """37326-LOG-IC-DIFF."""
    global WS_SEARCH_FROM, WS_SEARCH_TO, WS_IC_DIFF

    ws_ic_diff_rec: WS_IC_DIFF_REC = WS_IC_DIFF_REC()
    #     MOVE WS-SEARCH-FROM TO ICD-FROM
    ws_ic_diff_rec.ICD_FROM = WS_SEARCH_FROM
    #     MOVE WS-SEARCH-TO TO ICD-TO
    ws_ic_diff_rec.ICD_TO = WS_SEARCH_TO
    #     MOVE WS-IC-DIFF TO ICD-AMOUNT
    ws_ic_diff_rec.ICD_AMOUNT = WS_IC_DIFF
    #     WRITE IC-DIFF-RECORD FROM WS-IC-DIFF-REC
    write_record(IC_DIFF_RECORD, ws_ic_diff_rec)

def report_ic_differences():
    """37330-REPORT-IC-DIFFERENCES."""
    #     DISPLAY 'INTERCOMPANY RECONCILIATION COMPLETE'
    print('INTERCOMPANY RECONCILIATION COMPLETE')

    """37410-LOAD-NOSTRO-STATEMENT."""
    global WS_NOSTRO_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG

    #     MOVE ZEROES TO WS-NOSTRO-COUNT
    WS_NOSTRO_COUNT = 0
    #     PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        #  READ NOSTRO-STATEMENT-FILE INTO WS-NOSTRO-ITEM
        ws_nostro_item: WS_NOSTRO_ITEM | None = read_nostro_statement_file()
        if ws_nostro_item is None:
            #     AT END
            #        MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            #     NOT AT END
            #        ADD 1 TO WS-NOSTRO-COUNT
            WS_NOSTRO_COUNT += 1
        #  END-READ
    #  END-PERFORM
    #  MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def read_nostro_statement_file() -> WS_NOSTRO_ITEM | None:
    """Dummy function to simulate reading from NOSTRO-STATEMENT-FILE."""

    else:
        return None

    log_data_change()
    log_system_event()

def log_user_action():
    """38100-LOG-USER-ACTION."""
    global WS_AUDIT_ID, WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP, WS_USER_ID, WS_ACTION_TYPE, WS_SESSION_ID

    ws_audit_record: WS_AUDIT_RECORD = WS_AUDIT_RECORD()
    #     COMPUTE WS-AUDIT-ID = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999999999
    WS_AUDIT_ID = random_number() * 99999999999
    #     MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-AUDIT-TIMESTAMP
    WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP = datetime.now()
    #     MOVE WS-USER-ID TO WS-AUDIT-USER
    WS_AUDIT_USER = WS_USER_ID
    #     MOVE WS-ACTION-TYPE TO WS-AUDIT-ACTION
    WS_AUDIT_ACTION = WS_ACTION_TYPE
    #     MOVE WS-SESSION-ID TO WS-AUDIT-SESSION-ID
    WS_AUDIT_SESSION_ID = WS_SESSION_ID
    #     WRITE AUDIT-RECORD FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD
    write_record("AUDIT-RECORD", ws_audit_record)

    ws_audit_record: WS_AUDIT_RECORD = WS_AUDIT_RECORD()

    #     MOVE WS-USER-ID TO WS-AUDIT-USER
    WS_AUDIT_USER = WS_USER_ID
    #     MOVE 'UPDATE' TO WS-AUDIT-ACTION
    WS_AUDIT_ACTION = 'UPDATE'
    #     MOVE WS-TABLE-NAME TO WS-AUDIT-TABLE
    WS_AUDIT_TABLE = WS_TABLE_NAME
    #     MOVE WS-RECORD-KEY TO WS-AUDIT-KEY

    #     MOVE WS-NEW-VALUE TO WS-AUDIT-NEW-VALUE

def archive_audit_logs():
    """38400-ARCHIVE-AUDIT-LOGS."""
    global WS_END_OF_MONTH

    #     IF WS-END-OF-MONTH = 'Y'
    if WS_END_OF_MONTH == 'Y':
        #        PERFORM 38410-MOVE-TO-ARCHIVE
        move_to_archive()
        #        PERFORM 38420-COMPRESS-ARCHIVE
        compress_archive()
    #     END-IF

def move_to_archive():
    """38410-MOVE-TO-ARCHIVE."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP, WS_ARCHIVE_DATE

    #     PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        #  READ AUDIT-FILE INTO WS-AUDIT-RECORD
        ws_audit_record: WS_AUDIT_RECORD | None = read_audit_file()
        if ws_audit_record is None:
            #     AT END
            #        MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            #     NOT AT END
            #        IF WS-AUDIT-TIMESTAMP < WS-ARCHIVE-DATE
            if WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP < WS_ARCHIVE_DATE:
                #           WRITE ARCHIVE-AUDIT-RECORD
                #              FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD
                write_record("ARCHIVE-AUDIT-RECORD", ws_audit_record)
                #           DELETE AUDIT-FILE
                delete_audit_file()
            #        END-IF
        #  END-READ
    #  END-PERFORM
    #  MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def read_audit_file() -> WS_AUDIT_RECORD | None:
    """Dummy function to simulate reading from AUDIT-FILE."""
    if WS_EOF_FLAG == 'Y':
        return None
    else:
        return WS_AUDIT_RECORD()

def delete_audit_file():
    """Dummy function to simulate deleting AUDIT-FILE."""
    pass

    """38420-COMPRESS-ARCHIVE."""
    #     DISPLAY 'COMPRESSING AUDIT ARCHIVE'
    print('COMPRESSING AUDIT ARCHIVE')

    """39000-PERFORMANCE-MONITORING."""
    collect_metrics()
    analyze_performance()
    generate_alerts()
    optimize_resources()

def collect_metrics():
    """39100-COLLECT-METRICS."""
    cpu_metrics()
    memory_metrics()
    io_metrics()
    transaction_metrics()

def cpu_metrics():
    """39110-CPU-METRICS."""
    global WS_CPU_UTILIZATION, WS_CPU_ALERT

    #     CALL 'GETCPU' USING WS-CPU-UTILIZATION
    WS_CPU_UTILIZATION = get_cpu_utilization()
    #     IF WS-CPU-UTILIZATION > 80
    if WS_CPU_UTILIZATION > 80:
        #        MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CPU-ALERT
        WS_CPU_ALERT = 'Y'
    #     END-IF

def get_cpu_utilization() -> int:
    """Dummy function to simulate calling GETCPU."""
    return 81 # Simulate > 80%

def memory_metrics():
    """39120-MEMORY-METRICS."""
    global WS_MEMORY_UTILIZATION, WS_MEMORY_ALERT

    #     CALL 'GETMEM' USING WS-MEMORY-UTILIZATION
    WS_MEMORY_UTILIZATION = get_memory_utilization()
    #     IF WS-MEMORY-UTILIZATION > 85
    if WS_MEMORY_UTILIZATION > 85:
        #        MOVE 'Y' TO WS-MEMORY-ALERT
        WS_MEMORY_ALERT = 'Y'
    #     END-IF

def get_memory_utilization() -> int:
    """Dummy function to simulate calling GETMEM."""
    return 86 # Simulate > 85%

def io_metrics():
    """39130-IO-METRICS."""
    global WS_IO_WAIT_TIME, WS_IO_THRESHOLD, WS_IO_ALERT

    #     CALL 'GETIO' USING WS-IO-WAIT-TIME
    WS_IO_WAIT_TIME = get_io_wait_time()
    #     IF WS-IO-WAIT-TIME > WS-IO-THRESHOLD
    if WS_IO_WAIT_TIME > WS_IO_THRESHOLD:
        #        MOVE 'Y' TO WS-IO-ALERT
        WS_IO_ALERT = 'Y'
    #     END-IF

def get_io_wait_time() -> int:
    """Dummy function to simulate calling GETIO."""
    return 50 # Simulate exceeding threshold

def transaction_metrics():
    """39140-TRANSACTION-METRICS."""
    global WS_TPS, WS_AVG_RESPONSE, WS_TRANS_COUNT, WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS, WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME

    #     COMPUTE WS-TPS =
    #        WS-TRANS-COUNT / WS-ELAPSED-SECONDS
    WS_TPS = WS_TRANS_COUNT / WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS
    #     COMPUTE WS-AVG-RESPONSE =
    #        WS-TOTAL-RESPONSE-TIME / WS-TRANS-COUNT
    WS_AVG_RESPONSE = WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME / WS_TRANS_COUNT

def analyze_performance():
    """39200-ANALYZE-PERFORMANCE."""
    global WS_AVG_RESPONSE, WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLD, WS_PERF_DEGRADED, WS_TPS, WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLD, WS_THROUGHPUT_LOW

    #     IF WS-AVG-RESPONSE > WS-RESPONSE-THRESHOLD
    if WS_AVG_RESPONSE > WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLD:
        #        MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PERF-DEGRADED
        WS_PERF_DEGRADED = 'Y'
    #     END-IF
    #     IF WS-TPS < WS-MIN-TPS-THRESHOLD
    if WS_TPS < WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLD:
        #        MOVE 'Y' TO WS-THROUGHPUT-LOW
        WS_THROUGHPUT_LOW = 'Y'
    #     END-IF

def generate_alerts():
    """39300-GENERATE-ALERTS."""
    global WS_CPU_ALERT, WS_MEMORY_ALERT, WS_PERF_DEGRADED

    #     IF WS-CPU-ALERT = 'Y'
    if WS_CPU_ALERT == 'Y':
        #        PERFORM 39310-SEND-CPU-ALERT
        send_cpu_alert()
    #     END-IF
    #     IF WS-MEMORY-ALERT = 'Y'
    if WS_MEMORY_ALERT == 'Y':
        #        PERFORM 39320-SEND-MEMORY-ALERT
        send_memory_alert()
    #     END-IF
    #     IF WS-PERF-DEGRADED = 'Y'
    if WS_PERF_DEGRADED == 'Y':
        #        PERFORM 39330-SEND-PERF-ALERT
        send_perf_alert()
    #     END-IF

def send_cpu_alert():
    """39310-SEND-CPU-ALERT."""
    global WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT, WS_CPU_UTILIZATION

    #     MOVE 'HIGH-CPU' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'HIGH-CPU'
    #     MOVE 'EMAIL' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
    #     STRING 'ALERT: CPU utilization at ' DELIMITED SIZE
    #            WS-CPU-UTILIZATION DELIMITED SIZE
    #            '%' DELIMITED SIZE
    #        INTO WS-NOTIF-
